[[sep-rom-dv-verification-plan]]
== SEP Boot ROM Firmware Verification Plan

=== Overview

[cols=",",options="header"]
|===
|Field |Value
|Design Under Test |The production SEP Boot ROM (BL0), `hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod`, executing on `sep_wrapper` with the full VeeR EL2 core
|Platform |The `rom_boot` RTL target in `sep_sim_cfg.toml`: the full CPU plus the shared AXI slave agent on the SEP->SMC port
|Repository |`tt-oca-harness`
|Project |OCAH
|Framework |PyUVM on cocotb
|Primary simulator |Verilator
|Design specification |`hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/doc/rom.adoc` (SEP ROM Boot Architecture) and the chapters that `hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/doc/index.adoc` includes
|Status codes |`hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/include/status_values.h`
|Manifest layout |The `tt-oca-manifest` submodule at `hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/tools/tt-oca-manifest`
|Register sources |`hw/sys/sep/regs/` (SystemRDL) and the generated export `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/`
|Testbench architecture |<<sep-dv-testbench-architecture,SEP_TB_ARCH>>, Boot ROM Firmware Builds (<<_boot_rom_firmware_builds>>)
|How to run |`README.md` in this DV tree (`tools/dv/run_dv.py --dut sep --items rom_fw`)
|Tests |Every member of the `rom_fw` group in `testlists/all.toml`, defined in `testlists/rom_fw.toml`: 237 tests
|===

This plan is the technical contract for the Boot ROM firmware tests. It states
what each test drives, what it observes, which checkers can fail it, and which
requirements of the ROM specification this plan maps it to. The SEP hardware plan
(<<sep-dv-verification-plan>>) does not grade these tests, and this plan does
not grade the hardware tests.

The DUT is firmware. The RTL is the platform that executes it. A test proves a
ROM behaviour only through state that the ROM makes visible: the verdict and
status words in SEP cold scratch, the ROM virtual console, the SPI flash
transactions, the SMC-side memory and the registers that the ROM writes.

PASS needs positive evidence: a passing `results.xml` and named `CHK-<ID> PASS`
records in the leaf log (<<sep-rom-vplan-credit-rules>>). A clean simulator exit
is not a PASS.

=== Verification Strategy

[[sep-rom-vplan-stimulus]]
==== Stimulus

The ROM image is the production build. A test changes the inputs of the ROM,
never the ROM itself.

* *ROM image.* `+sep_boot_rom_hex` loads `bootrom/prod/build/boot_rom.vmem`, the
  `boot_rom` firmware profile (SECURE_DMA RX drain).
  `sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test` loads `build_pio/boot_rom.vmem`, the
  `boot_rom_pio` profile (CPU programmed-I/O drain).
  `sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test`, `sep_rom_clear_variant_test` and the `scrub`
  and `iccm_clear` zero-fill fault entries load `build_clear/boot_rom.vmem`, the
  `boot_rom_clear` profile (full SEP SRAM scrub and full ICCM clear).
* *Manifest and payload.* The `oca-images` and `decrypt_negative_images` steps
  pack the OCA images, for example `oca_non_secure_boot.bin`,
  `oca_secure_boot.bin` and `oca_smc_mem.hex`.
  `env/sep_manifest_mutate.py` and `env/sep_payload_mutate.py` plant one
  defect in the packed bytes at run time and re-seal what the scenario needs
  sealed. `env/sep_rom_key_slots.py` binds a slot to a ROM key and re-signs it
  with the test keys in `bootrom/prod/tests/signing_keys/`.
* *SPI flash.* `OcahSpiFlash` (`ocah_spi_vip`) serves the combined image. The
  primary manifest is at flash offset `0x1000` and the backup at `0x41000`.
  `+sep_boot_from_spi` sets the `primary_chiplet` strap so the ROM boots from
  SPI.
* *SMC side.* `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` answers the SEP->SMC AXI port.
  `env/sep_smc_mem.py` preloads the SMC scratch words, the DFX status word, the
  strap words and the SMC-SRAM manifest image (`+sep_smc_mem_hex`,
  `+sep_dft_status`, `+sep_straps_lo`, `+sep_straps_hi`, `+sep_smc_scratch8`).
* *eFuse.* The bench senses a real OTP image: either the `SepEfuseImage`
  builder (`env/sep_efuse_image.py`) or a committed preload from
  `tb/efuse_preloads/efuse_configurations/` (`+sep_efuse_preload`). No test uses
  `+skip_fuse_sense`, because the ROM lifecycle policy needs the `LC_STATE` that
  only the real fuse sense writes.
* *Warm reset.* `+sep_cold_scratch7` seeds the warm-handler slot and
  `+sep_iccm_word` plants the handler instruction.
* *Fault injection.* `+sep_otbn_cmd_drop` makes OTBN ignore its commands and
  `+sep_dma_host_stall` wedges the secure DMA host port. `+zero_fill_fault`
  makes one secure DMA zero fill end in a bus error.
* *Entropy.* `+esrc_noise_force` with `env/sep_esrc_noise.py` drives the ESRC
  noise input, because the ring oscillators do not oscillate in simulation.

[[sep-rom-vplan-checking]]
==== Checking

* *Verdict.* `cold_scratch[0]` carries `TEST_PASS_CODE` or `TEST_FAIL_CODE`
  (`env/sep_verdict.py`). A boot test requires PASS from BL1. A refusal test
  requires FAIL or a terminal status word with no BL1 verdict.
* *Status word.* `cold_scratch[1]` carries the encoded STATUS / WARNING / ERROR
  word. Expected words come from `status_values.h`.
* *Console.* The ROM has no UART. `env/sep_rom_console.py` decodes the virtual
  console on `cold_scratch[2]`, and `env/sep_oca_console.py` models the tokens
  that one slot attempt can print. A test requires its markers in order and
  forbids the markers of the paths it excludes.
* *Transport.* `env/sep_spi_slot_evidence.py` reads the flash BFM transaction
  log, so a test can prove which flash address the ROM read and what the device
  answered. The console proves only what the ROM intended to read.
* *Halt.* A terminal test requires the terminal status word and then a PC span
  of at most 64 bytes for a fixed window. Retirement count cannot tell a spin from
  forward progress. PC locality can.
* *Registers and memory.* Tests read the SMC-side words that the ROM writes
  (`scratch[10]`, `CPU_CTRL.RESET_CTRL`), the demotion registers, the fuse
  read-lock state, and SEP SRAM residue after a failover.
* *Evidence.* `sep_base_test` counts each `CHK-<ID> PASS` (or `OK`) record that
  the leaf logs, and fails a leaf that logs no record of its own.

[[sep-rom-vplan-bringup]]
==== Bring-up

. Build or select the eFuse image and write it as the sensed OTP.
. Set the straps: `+sep_boot_from_spi` for the SPI paths, the strap plusargs
  for the other arms.
. Load the SPI flash or the SMC-SRAM image, after the test applies its
  mutation.
. Some tests also stage `boot_rom.itcm.hex` and `boot_rom.dtcm.hex` through
  `tcm_load_i` before reset, and their member rows name it as a DV shortcut.
. Release the VeeR core at the Boot ROM base and let real fuse sense complete.
. Poll for the BL1 verdict or the terminal status word up to the cycle limit of
  the test, and abort when the core has retired no instruction by the end of the
  no-boot window.
. Judge the observables and log the `CHK-<ID>` records.

[[sep-rom-vplan-expansion]]
==== Scenario Expansion

A mutation test plants one defect kind, in the primary slot, the backup slot or
both:

* A defect in the primary slot expects a WARNING for that slot, a rotation to
  the backup and a BL1 boot from the backup.
* A defect in the backup slot, behind a primary that is also refused, expects
  the backup's own refusal code and a terminal halt.
* A defect in both slots expects both refusals and a terminal halt.

Every test runs on `seed = 1`, except `sep_key_revocation_bitmap_random_test`
(`seed = 17`). `sep_key_revocation_bitmap_random_test` and
`sep_firmware_payload_exceeds_slot_window_random_test` each draw one value from
their seed per regression.

=== Naming Rules

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Item |Rule
|Platform and path test |`sep_rom_<path>_<scenario>_test`, for example `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test`
|Manifest and payload mutation |`sep_firmware_<scenario>_test`, where the scenario names the slot that carries the defect (`primary` or `backup`), for example `sep_firmware_manifest_backup_invalid_chiplet_id_test`
|Feature test |`sep_<feature>_<scenario>_test`, for example `sep_warm_reset_invalid_hang_test`
|Family base |`sep_<family>_base.py` or `sep_<family>_defect.py` in `cocotb/tests/rom_fw/`. A base is not registered as a test
|Checker |`CHK-<AREA>-<CLAIM>`, logged as `CHK-<ID> PASS` after the check
|Testlist entry |`name` equals the class name, and `module` is `rom_fw.<file>`
|===

These 6 entries have a `module` file name that differs from the test name:

* `sep_firmware_demotion_bl2_request_prod_end_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_demotion_decision_unauth_flag_0_prod_end_test`.
* `sep_firmware_demotion_bl1_disable_over_bl2_request_prod_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_demotion_decision_unauth_flag_0_prod_sel_bit_set_test`.
* `sep_firmware_demotion_bl2_request_prod_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_demotion_decision_unauth_flag_0_prod_test`.
* `sep_firmware_demotion_bl1_disable_secure_prod_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_demotion_decision_unauth_flag_30_prod_sel_bit_set_test`.
* `sep_firmware_payload_correct_sep_sram_4kb_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_payload_correct_ext_4kb_test`.
* `sep_firmware_payload_correct_sep_sram_29kb_test` is in `rom_fw.sep_firmware_payload_correct_ext_29kb_test`.

In <<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>>, 18 more entries run a module named for their leaf, not for the entry:
`sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test_hold`, the seven `sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_*` entries, the three
`sep_rom_smc_manifest_offset_invalid_test_*` entries, the two `sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_*`
entries, `sep_rom_rsa_exponent_non_f4_refuse_test_f4_signed` and the four
`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_*` entries. A plusarg in each entry selects its case.

In <<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>>, the four `sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_*_test` entries run
`rom_fw.sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_test`, and `+zero_fill_fault` selects the clear site.

=== BFM Policy

[cols="1,1,2",options="header"]
|===
|Interface / Protocol |VIP / Model |Purpose
|SPI flash |`OcahSpiFlash` (`ocah_spi_vip`) |Serves the combined OCA image and records every transaction for `env/sep_spi_slot_evidence.py`
|SEP->SMC AXI |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` with `env/sep_smc_mem.py` |Answers the ROM's SMC scratch, DFX status, strap and SMC-SRAM accesses from a flat memory that the test preloads
|eFuse / OTP |Generic eFuse bank model, `SepEfuseImage`, `+sep_efuse_preload` |Supplies the sensed lifecycle, `SBOOT_DIS`, key digests, revocation bitmap and identity fuses
|ESRC noise |`env/sep_esrc_noise.py` with `+esrc_noise_force` |Drives the decorrelator noise input so the entropy chain passes its health tests
|ROM console |`env/sep_rom_console.py`, `env/sep_oca_console.py` |Decodes the virtual console on `cold_scratch[2]` and models the legal token order
|Verdict and status |`env/sep_verdict.py`, `scratch_cold_probe_o` |Reads `cold_scratch[0]` and `cold_scratch[1]`
|CPU trace |`cpu_trace_valid_o`, `cpu_trace_addr_o`, `env/sep_cpu_trace_monitor.py`, `env/sep_boot_scoreboard.py` |The monitor samples the retired PCs. The scoreboard requires retirement at many distinct PCs, and the terminal tests bound the PC span of a halt from the same trace
|===

A model generates stimulus or observes passively. A test judges the ROM through
a checker that can fail. Activity on an interface is not proof.

[[sep-rom-vplan-scope]]
=== Verification Scope

This plan claims Boot ROM behaviour from the release of the SEP core reset to
the jump into BL1, as `rom.adoc` defines it, on the `rom_boot` target.

[cols="1,2",options="header"]
|===
|Behaviour |Disposition
|Boot paths: SMC-SRAM manifest, OpenTitan SPI (DMA and PIO drain), secondary chiplet |Verified by <<sep-rom-vplan-smc-sram-boot>>, <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>, <<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>>
|Warm-reset dispatch and the pre-C memory-repair gate |Verified by <<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> and <<sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate>>
|Lifecycle validation, `SBOOT_DIS`, chiplet debug lock and the secure-boot decision |Verified by the fuse and lifecycle groups
|OCA manifest validation, the two-slot retry and failure convergence |Verified by the manifest groups
|Key authorization, revocation, anti-rollback and RSA-3072 verification |Verified by the key groups and <<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>>
|Payload hash, decryption, TOC rules and BL1 placement |Verified by the payload groups
|Demotion decision and the demotion registers |Verified by the demotion groups
|Fuse-secret read locks before hand-off |Verified by <<sep-rom-vplan-fuse-lock>>
|BL1 and later stages |Out of scope. A BL1 stub reports the verdict only
|SEP hardware: fabric, crypto IP, eFuse controller, lifecycle controller |Owned by <<sep-dv-verification-plan>>
|SMC Boot ROM and the SMC side of the scratch protocol |Owned by the SMC plans. This bench models the SMC as a memory responder
|SPI controller, OTBN, AES and HMAC IP-level behaviour |Owned by their IP testbenches. This plan observes their use by the ROM
|PQC signatures, ECDSA acceptance and multi-signer verification |Not implemented by this ROM generation (`rom.adoc`, Implementation-Status Matrix)
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-traceability]]
=== Scope and Traceability

[[sep-rom-vplan-source-of-truth]]
==== Source of truth

[cols="2,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Question |Source of truth |Disposition of the other documents
|What the ROM must do |`hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/doc/rom.adoc` and its `SEP-ROM-<AREA>-<NNN>` requirements |This plan cites them. A plan statement that contradicts the specification is a plan defect
|Status and error codes |`hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/include/status_values.h` |Tests take expected status words from it
|Manifest and TOC layout |The `tt-oca-manifest` submodule (`oca_layout.h`, `constants.py`) |The mutators resolve offsets from the packer, not from constants in the tests
|Register layouts |The SystemRDL under `hw/sys/sep/regs/` and `hw/sys/smc/regs/` |`sep_reg_meta.sym()` reads the generated SEP export. `env/sep_smc_mem.py` holds the SMC word addresses that the ROM reads through the SEP->SMC window
|Which tests exist |`testlists/rom_fw.toml` and the `rom_fw` group in `testlists/all.toml` |The Test Procedures document every member of `rom_fw`. A name in one and not the other is a defect in whichever lacks it
|Regression command |`README.md` in this DV tree |This plan states the intent, not the shell commands
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-live-groups]]
==== Live groups versus credit

[cols="1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Role
|`rom_fw_smoke` |Bring-up of the four boot paths and the hand-off magic: `sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test`, `sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test`, `sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test` (`expected_count = 5`)
|`rom_fw` |The credit denominator of this plan: 237 tests. The group has no `expected_count` (<<sep-rom-vplan-checklist>>)
|`all`, `cpu`, `cpu_stub` |Not members. They belong to <<sep-dv-verification-plan>>
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-credit-rules]]
==== Credit rules

* Requirement credit needs a test in `rom_fw` that maps to the requirement, a
  passing `results.xml`, and the `CHK-<ID> PASS` records that the test's
  procedure group names. This plan records no run. Each matrix row is a
  planned vehicle until a run supplies that evidence.
* Expected values come from `rom.adoc`, `status_values.h`, the OCA layout and,
  for the status-word mapping and the demotion encoding, `oca_boot.c` and
  `measurement.h`.
* A refusal test needs a positive control: the ROM reached the stage that
  refuses (for example `MANIFEST_OK` or `RSA_VERIFY_OK` before a payload
  refusal), and the planted defect is the only defect in the slot.
* A refusal at the wrong stage, or with a different code, fails the test.
  "Any refusal" is not a pass.
* A test that forces a signal, drops a command or stages memory through a
  backdoor declares it in its procedure group. The forced value is stimulus,
  never a ROM-generated result.
* This plan maps each test to requirements from the behaviour that the test
  judges.

[[sep-rom-vplan-summary]]
=== Scenario-Expanded Test Summary

One row per test, in `rom_fw.toml` order within each area. The procedure group
holds the stimulus, the checkers and the requirements.

==== Boot paths and transport

[cols="3,2,4",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Procedure group |Expected outcome
|`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test` (smoke) |<<sep-rom-vplan-smc-sram-boot>> |boots: BL1 PASS verdict in cold_scratch[0] after SMC_MEM_CHK, ROM_HASH_VERIFIED, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, MEAS_BOOT_STATE_OK, BL1, FUSE_CHK.
|`sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` (smoke) |<<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> |boots: BL1 PASS verdict with BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK; WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST absent.
|`sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test` (smoke) |<<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> |boots; sram_word0_probe_o low 32 bits read back as b"OCAC" after hand-off.
|`sep_iccm_pad_sram_top_intact_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> |boots as `sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test`; the last SEP SRAM word still holds its pre-boot sentinel at BL1_COPIED.
|`sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test` (smoke) |<<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> |boots (same markers and PASS verdict as the DMA base).
|`sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test` (smoke) |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |boots after PUBK_AUTHORIZED, ENTROPY_OK, RSA_EXEC and RSA_VERIFY_OK; SBOOT_OFF never printed.
|`sep_spi_detect_success_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect>> |boots from primary 0x1000; device returned OCAC there and served no read inside the backup span.
|`sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect>> |primary refused with OCA_FAIL_MAGIC (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002); backup at 0x41000 boots to BL1; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and the SPI-init-failure route absent.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect>> |Word 0 holds the erased primary residue, then all words read 0 after MANIFEST_ERR (BAD_MAGIC) and before MANIFEST_BACKUP/backup SRC, then the backup fetch re-dirties word 0; boots to BL1 PASS.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect>> |terminal: two MANIFEST_ERR=OCA_FAIL_MAGIC, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, cold_scratch[1] 0x0F010006 and last non-ring status 0x0F010213, FAIL verdict, no boot.
|`sep_boot_recovery_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>> |BOOT_RECOVERY arm: WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, MANIFEST_SRC=0x40061000, MANIFEST_OK and BL1 FUSE_CHK, PASS verdict; BOOT_SPI, BOOT_SECONDARY, SPI_INIT markers absent and zero flash reads.
|`sep_rotate_update_set_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect>> |first and only slot read is 0x41000 (MANIFEST_BACKUP, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000) and it boots; MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_PRIMARY and any MANIFEST_ERR= absent; no read in the primary span.
|`sep_firmware_bl1_ver_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |primary accepted: MFST_VER/FUSE_VER echoes (0x00000001) before RSA_EXEC, FUSE_VER= exactly twice, PUBK_SEL= before FUSE_VER=; boots with no backup read and no MANIFEST_ERR=.
|`sep_rom_oca_encrypted_boot_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |Boots BL1 with the secure-boot markers plus DECRYPT_OK and PAYLOAD_OK; DECRYPT_NO_SECRET, DECRYPT_CLASS_KEY_EMPTY, KDF_FAIL, AES_DEC_FAIL, MANIFEST_ERR= and HMAC/SHA reject markers absent.
|`sep_rom_oca_otp_key_boot_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |Boots BL1 with PUBK_AUTHORIZED and RSA_VERIFY_OK; PUBK_OTP_EMPTY and PUBK_UNAUTHORIZED absent.
|`sep_firmware_encrypted_boot_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |Single primary attempt accepted in order PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK, DECRYPT_OK, PAYLOAD_OK; DECRYPT_OK once; boots BL1.
|`sep_boot_measurement_golden_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |Primary boots; MEAS_BOOT_STATE_OK, BL0S_BOOT_PCR=, BL0S_OK printed and the PCR equals the golden; backup never read.
|`sep_rsa_verify_redundant_compare_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |One RSA_EXEC, RSA_CMP1 and RSA_CMP2 once each, in order RSA_EXEC, RSA_CMP1, RSA_CMP2, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK on the primary only; boots.
|`sep_firmware_primary_manifest_major_version_valid_minor_0_length_correct_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot>> |Primary accepted in order OCA_BODY= .. BL1_JUMP=; one SPI read is exactly the body, no read falls outside body or payload, backup never read; boots BL1.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>> |one attempt at 0x40061000 accepted: OCA_BODY=, MFST_VER=, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, SBOOT_OFF, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=, BL1 FUSE_CHK in order; cold_scratch[0] PASS; no PUBK_\*/RSA_EXEC/MANIFEST_ERR=.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>> |complete valid SMC image at 0x40061004 refused before OCA_BODY= with MANIFEST_ALIGN_FAIL and OCA_BOOT_ERR_MANIFEST_ALIGNMENT (0x00030108); one attempt, FAIL verdict, halt.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_invalid_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>> |READY with scratch[8]=0xFFFFFFFF refused before any slot with MANIFEST_OFFSET_INVALID and OCA_BOOT_ERR_MANIFEST_OFFSET_INVALID (0x00030109); no MANIFEST_SRC=, FAIL verdict, halt.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_primary_manifest_invalid_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet>> |one attempt at 0x40065000 refused MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002, then MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030002; cold_scratch[1] ERROR word; FAIL verdict; halt.
|===

[cols="3,2,4",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Procedure group |Expected outcome
|`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>> |clear-enabled ROM boots to BL1 PASS; every SEP SRAM word reads zero at SRAM_CLR_OK and every ICCM word outside the BL1 copy span reads zero at BL1_COPIED; ICCM_PAD= absent.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_pad_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>> |faulted ICCM pad: BL1_DST=ICCM then ICCM_PAD_FAIL; ICCM_PAD=, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR and ERROR SEP_MSG_BL1_BAD_ADDR on cold_scratch[1]; no PASS verdict.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_retry_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>> |faulted SEP SRAM clear after the erased primary: MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002, SRAM_RETRY_CLR_FAIL, MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030100 in order; no backup staged; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR and ERROR SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED; no PASS verdict.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_scrub_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>> |faulted SEP SRAM scrub (clear-enabled ROM): SRAM_SCRUB_LEN= then SRAM_CLR_FAIL; SRAM_CLR_OK and MANIFEST_SRC= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR; no PASS verdict.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_iccm_clear_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear>> |faulted full ICCM clear (clear-enabled ROM): SRAM_CLR_OK then ICCM_CLR_FAIL; ICCM_CLR_FULL and MANIFEST_SRC= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR; no PASS verdict.

|===
|Test |Procedure group |Expected outcome
|`sep_firmware_mbist_fail_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate>> |terminal ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED: cold_scratch[1] WARN 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001, SMC scratch[10]=0xFFFFFFFD, silent console, PC spin <=64 B.
|`sep_scratch_7_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> |warm jump: cold_scratch[1]=0x01010068 (WARM_RESET_JUMP), cold_scratch[7] 0xC0000100->0x0, instruction retired at 0xC0000100, no console.
|`sep_firmware_mbist_only_fail_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate>> |scratch[10]=0x00000012; cold_scratch[1] 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001 (ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED); silent console; PC spins; no boot.
|`sep_firmware_mbist_pass_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate>> |boots; cold_scratch[1] shows PRESTART_DONE 0x80010056 and never WARN 0x08010219 or ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED 0x0F01D001; SMC scratch[10] stays 0; SMC_MEM_CHK and CHIP_ID= printed.
|`sep_mbist_fail_skip_fuse_ignored_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate>> |STATUS_RPT bit 2 blown, still terminal ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED: cold_scratch[1] WARN 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001 and no later change, SMC scratch[10]=0xFFFFFFFD, PC only in the halt spin, no C-runtime console marker.
|`sep_warm_reset_invalid_hang_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> |terminal hang: cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010069 (WARM_RESET_HANG), no 0x01010068, no cold-boot words or poison, silent console, PC spin <=64 B.
|`sep_warm_reset_below_range_hang_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> |cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010069 (WARM_RESET_HANG); no 0x01010068 jump, no BOOTROM_START/PRESTART_DONE, no 0xFFFFFFFF poison, silent console; PC spins within 64 B.
|`sep_warm_reset_unarmed_cold_boot_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> |cold_scratch[7] 0 -> 0xFFFFFFFF; cold_scratch[1] shows 0x80010044 then 0x80010056; no WARM_RESET_JUMP or WARM_RESET_HANG.
|`sep_warm_reset_bad_target_exception_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset>> |0x01010068 (WARM_RESET_JUMP) then 0x0F010028 (GENERAL_EXCEPTION); mepc 0xC0000200 and mcause 2 in later cold_scratch[1] words; cold_scratch[0]=0xDEADBEEF; no BOOTROM_START, no poison.

|Test |Procedure group |Expected outcome
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |boots to the BL1 PASS verdict; the observer's retirement, status, console, watch, tracker and verdict records match the spec values and the independent trace count.

|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |up to the hand-off mret: no outbound-filter or WDT register write, no interrupt taken or enabled, mie and mstatus.MIE cleared before the PMP programming, sp and gp in DCCM; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_dccm_scrub_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |with DCCM errors planted before reset, every DCCM read returns a value this boot wrote, and [DCCM base, `__stack_top`) is zero-written before `rom_main`.
|`sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |every SEP SRAM byte and ICCM bank row is zero-written, and the probed SRAM residue words read zero, before the first manifest read, on the clear-enabled ROM build.

|`sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_rma_sip_0x3` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |raw LC 0x3 recorded and reported as above; BL1 PASS.

|`sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_rma_chiplet_0x7` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |raw LC 0x7 recorded and reported as above; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_prod_end` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |raw LC 0x8 recorded and reported as above; the unsigned default image is then refused and the run ends on the FAIL verdict.
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |before the "PMP programmed" status: RLB set before the first locked rule, every rule locked, MMWP set after the rules, no catch-all, windows equal tbl:pmp_regions, execute on the Boot ROM alone.
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |one release write adds execute to the BL1-region rule under RLB after the hand-off progress codes; no other CSR write before mepc; mepc, fence.i, fence, mret to the TOC entry.
|`sep_rom_demote_after_lock_order_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |DEMOTE_1 leaves its reset value only after all six fuse-secret read-lock bits are set; the inherited O5 demotion checks hold.
|`sep_rom_version_hash_report_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |the console carries BL0_VERSION and the golden ROM SHA-256; ROM_HASH and two INFO_EXT prefix words on cold_scratch[1]; all within [S05].
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |two enrollments, slot 0 then slot 1; slot 0 after a full read of the hashed ROM region that follows [S14] and [S10]; slot 1 after MANIFEST_VALIDATED; the inherited measurement checks hold.
|`sep_rom_smc_scratch_sanity_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |the four spec patterns written to SMC scratch[7] and read back equal, then scratch[7] restored to zero; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_ready_wait_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |no scratch[8] or SMC SRAM read while MANIFEST_READY is low; after the release a scratch[9] poll, a scratch[8] read and the manifest fetch at the published offset; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_offset_invalid_test_window_end` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |scratch[8] = 0x00100000 (first byte past the window): the single slot fails as above.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_offset_invalid_test_window_straddle` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |scratch[8] = 0x000FFFF8: the single slot fails as above.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_offset_invalid_test_manifest_straddle` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |scratch[8] = 0x000FF008 (manifest end crosses the window by 8 bytes): the single slot fails as above; the reads before the refusal are logged only, because `rom.adoc` and `smc-coordination.adoc` disagree on whether the ROM checks the window before it reads.
|`sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_straddle` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |ring header across the window end: no SMC write outside the window or to the header, STATUS_REPORTING_INVALID on cold_scratch[1], BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_bad_header` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |ring head equal to its entry count: no SMC write outside the window or the declared ring, STATUS_REPORTING_INVALID on cold_scratch[1], BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_status_ring_severity_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |the final 8-entry ring equals expected_ring() of the offered statuses: INFO marker at 3 free slots, WARN marker at 2, ERROR in the last slot.
|`sep_rom_oca_library_callback_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |the callback table wires all eight spec roles to ROM functions; the library entry retires before MANIFEST_VALIDATED and calls the sha256, verify_signature, revocation and security-version callbacks from library text; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_entropy_health_fail_terminal_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED, a health-test console marker, then ERROR 0x0F01F009 and 0xDEADBEEF; no backup attempt and no SPI access; halt.
|`sep_rom_entropy_lock_order_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |FIPS_LOCK after the last health poll and before the first entropy consumer; source lock after the Instantiate and before the hand-off; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_no_fuse_program_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |a newer manifest security version boots BL1; the OTP bank is unchanged at hand-off and at the end, and no PROGRAM command reaches the eFuse shim.
|`sep_rom_rsa_exponent_non_f4_refuse_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |primary key with exponent 5 (signature valid under (n, 5)) refused with a MANIFEST_ERR= code; the backup boots to BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_rsa_exponent_non_f4_refuse_test_f4_signed` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |primary key with exponent 5 (signature valid under (n, 65537)) refused with a MANIFEST_ERR= code; the backup boots to BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_rsa_key_direct_otbn_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |the CPU writes the whole modulus into OTBN DMEM before EXECUTE; the Key Manager key paths, sideload keys, CPU and mailbox stay idle; BL1 PASS.
|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |both unsigned encrypted slots refused, `bl0_state.error_code` one of the two codes the spec names for this refusal, no decryption, ERROR 0x0F010213 and 0xDEADBEEF; halt.
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_success` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |boots; no 16-byte window of key material in DCCM when the decryption stage returns and at the hand-off mret.
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_init_fail` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |AES-init failure exit: no key material in DCCM at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict.
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_kdf_fail` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |key-derivation failure exit: no key material in DCCM at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict.
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_dec_fail` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |AES cipher-setup failure exit (CTRL update rejected before the key load): no key material in DCCM at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict.
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |<<sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded>> |each slot's manifest read made 2 to 4 times, the backup tried after the primary, one MANIFEST_LOAD_START per slot attempt, then ERROR MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED and 0xDEADBEEF; the partial frame each faulted read lands is not used; halt.

[[sep-rom-vplan-procedures]]
=== Test Procedures

Each group starts from the common bring-up (<<sep-rom-vplan-bringup>>). A group
with a base class states the base's procedure and checkers once. Its members
table states what each test changes, what it expects, the checkers it adds and
the requirements that this plan maps it to. Every test passes only when every checker of its
group and every checker it adds passes, and the evidence floor sees at least one
PASS record of its own.

==== Boot paths and transport
[[sep-rom-vplan-smc-sram-boot]]
===== SMC-SRAM manifest boot

*Tests:* 1.

*Procedure:* each member is an independent test on the common bring-up. It applies the stimulus in its row, waits for the outcome in its row, and judges it with the checkers in its row.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test` |TEST_DEV eFuse image; manifest+BL1 served from SMC SRAM (+sep_smc_mem_hex); no +sep_boot_from_spi. |boots: BL1 PASS verdict in cold_scratch[0]; the console shows SMC_MEM_CHK, ROM_HASH_VERIFIED, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, MEAS_BOOT_STATE_OK, BL1 and FUSE_CHK. |`CHK-ROM-STAGES`, `CHK-VERDICT` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-050`, `SEP-ROM-ATT-060`
|===

*Added checker contracts:*

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-ROM-STAGES` |All seven ROM/BL1 stage markers are present on the scratch console.
|`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-VERDICT` |cold_scratch[0] carries the PASS verdict; SepBootScoreboard requires fw_pass and PC advance.
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot]]
===== OpenTitan SPI boot and its variants

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test.py`.

*Objective:* The production Boot ROM fetches an OCA manifest and BL1 from a SPI flash model over the OpenTitan SPI host (SECURE_DMA drain), validates them and hands off to BL1, which reports PASS in cold_scratch[0].

*Procedure:*

. Build a zero eFuse image stamped TEST_DEV (members may select a committed +sep_efuse_preload instead) and write it as the sensed OTP and golden.
. Set STRAPS_LO[25] primary_chiplet through +sep_boot_from_spi and start the ESRC noise driver.
. Load build/oca_non_secure_boot.bin (members: oca_secure_boot.bin) into OcahSpiFlash at offset 0, primary manifest 0x1000 and backup 0x41000, after the mutate_flash_image hook.
. Release the VeeR CPU at the Boot ROM base (+sep_boot_rom_hex) with no TCM staging.
. Poll cold_scratch[0] for the ROM/BL1 verdict up to 24M cycles; abort if no instruction retires in 200k cycles.
. Capture ROM virt-console lines and the flash BFM transaction log; dump cold scratch.
. Assert every required marker (BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK) is present and WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST is absent.
. Scoreboard check_phase requires PC advance and fw_done && fw_pass (fw_pass low when expect_fw_pass=False); the check_transport hook judges device-side evidence.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required console marker appears in the ROM console.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |cold_scratch[0] carries a PASS verdict before the cycle limit and the scoreboard asserts fw_done, fw_pass and distinct-PC advance.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts required_markers is non-empty and the console is non-empty; MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000 is the transport evidence that the SMC-SRAM branch (absolute 0x4006xxxx) cannot print, and the scoreboard requires distinct fetch PCs.

*Observation:* ROM virt-console markers, cold_scratch[0] verdict via scratch_cold_probe_o, CPU trace distinct PCs, and (in members) flash BFM read transactions and served bytes.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` |+sep_boot_from_spi (STRAPS_LO[25]); unsigned oca_non_secure_boot.bin preloaded into OcahSpiFlash at flash 0; TEST_DEV OTP. DV shortcut: esrc_noise_task drives the ESRC noise port (no +esrc_noise_force plusarg in this entry). |boots: BL1 PASS verdict with BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK; WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST absent. |`CHK-ROM-PATH`, `CHK-VERDICT` |`SEP-ROM-SPI-010`, `SEP-ROM-MAN-050`
|`sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test` |Unmodified non-secure image under default TEST_DEV; additionally requires ICCM_PAD= and BL1_JUMP= console markers. |boots; sram_word0_probe_o low 32 bits read back as b"OCAC" after hand-off. |`CHK-MANIFEST-INTACT` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-050`
|`sep_iccm_pad_sram_top_intact_test` |Runs `sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test` and keeps its checks; the sentinel 0xC0FFEE5AA5EEFF0C is written to the last SEP SRAM word before reset and read back. DV shortcut: VPI backdoor write and read of the SEP SRAM macro array via sep_public_scope.vlt; Verilator only. |boots as the parent; the last SEP SRAM word still holds the sentinel when BL1_COPIED reaches the console, after the [S29] ICCM pad and the BL1 copy. |`CHK-SRAM-TOP-INTACT` |None: no `rom.adoc` requirement states it (see the contract)
|`sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test` |+sep_boot_rom_hex selects build_pio/boot_rom.vmem (BOOT_OT_SPI_USE_PIO=1); flash image is the shared build/oca_non_secure_boot.bin. |boots (same markers and PASS verdict as the DMA base). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SPI-010`

*Added checker contracts:*

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` |`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required marker BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK and PAYLOAD_OK appears.
|`sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` |`CHK-VERDICT` |cold_scratch[0] carries the BL1 PASS verdict.
|`sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test` |`CHK-MANIFEST-INTACT` |SEP SRAM word 0 equals MANIFEST_MAGIC (OCAC) after hand-off.
|`sep_iccm_pad_sram_top_intact_test` |`CHK-SRAM-TOP-INTACT` |The last SEP SRAM word, sampled when BL1_COPIED reaches the console, still equals the sentinel written before reset. No `rom.adoc` sentence states that the pad or the BL1 copy leaves SEP SRAM unchanged. The nearest are "Every clear is a secure DMA zero fill, which writes zeros without reading memory, so all 256 KiB are available to the manifest body, the payload and BL1's permitted load span." and "It sets the engine's enabled memory range, which bounds every zero-fill write, to the whole of that region". The check samples one word only.
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-secure-boot]]
===== Signed RSA-3072 secure boot

*Tests:* 8.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test.py`. `sep_firmware_bl1_ver_test` runs the <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> procedure on the signed image with its own markers and checkers, so the group checker `CHK-OTBN-EDN` and the PUBK_AUTHORIZED and ENTROPY_OK markers apply to the other seven members.

*Objective:* A signed RSA-3072 OCA image fetched over the OpenTitan SPI host boots BL1 only after key authorization, entropy bring-up and an OTBN RSA verify on the real EDN path.

*Procedure:*

. Build the default eFuse image (zero image stamped TEST_DEV) unless the member overrides build_efuse_image, and write it.
. Start the ESRC noise driver (+esrc_noise_force) and the ROM console capture.
. Preload OcahSpiFlash with oca_secure_boot.bin (primary manifest 0x1000, payload 0x2000) and seed the SPI boot strap.
. Start the OTBN EDN monitor on crypto_edn_req_o/crypto_edn_ack_o.
. Boot VeeR from the Boot ROM and poll cold_scratch[0] until the BL1 PASS verdict.
. Assert required markers BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, ENTROPY_OK, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK and forbid WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST and SBOOT_OFF.
. Assert OTBN requested URND and every RND/URND request episode was acknowledged with no drop or stray ack.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-VERDICT` |Inherited from sep_base_test.poll_boot: firmware completes with PASS through cold_scratch[0].
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Inherited: each required marker observed; forbidden markers (WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, SBOOT_OFF) absent.
|`CHK-OTBN-EDN` |OTBN requested URND and every observed RND/URND request was acknowledged on the real EDN path.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Non-empty required_markers and console are asserted, SBOOT_OFF is forbidden to catch a silent downgrade, and an OTBN URND request must be observed on crypto_edn_req_o.

*Observation:* ROM console markers, cold_scratch[0] verdict, crypto_edn_req_o/crypto_edn_ack_o handshake.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. Signed build/oca_secure_boot.bin (dev0 key, ROM slot 0, secure_boot_control bit 0 set) under the default TEST_DEV zero eFuse image. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |boots with PUBK_AUTHORIZED, ENTROPY_OK, RSA_EXEC and RSA_VERIFY_OK on the console; SBOOT_OFF never printed; OTBN requests URND and every RND/URND request is acknowledged. |`CHK-OTBN-EDN` |`SEP-ROM-SB-020`, `SEP-ROM-SB-090`, `SEP-ROM-ENT-010`
|`sep_firmware_bl1_ver_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_bl1ver36_spread.toml with BL1_VERSION low 128 bits = 0x0000000F_00000007_00000003_00000001; both slots' security_version set to the same value, re-sealed and re-signed. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary accepted: MFST_VER/FUSE_VER echoes (0x00000001) before RSA_EXEC, FUSE_VER= exactly twice, PUBK_SEL= before FUSE_VER=; boots with no backup read and no MANIFEST_ERR=. |`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE`, `CHK-STIMULUS-BOUNDARY`, `CHK-ROLLBACK-BOUNDARY`, `CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |`SEP-ROM-SB-070`
|`sep_rom_oca_encrypted_boot_test` |oca_encrypted_boot.bin; CLASS_KEY words 0x03020100..0x1F1E1D1C written by the TB into the default TEST_DEV eFuse image. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim); CLASS_KEY set_words into the TB eFuse image rather than a committed preload |Boots BL1 with the secure-boot markers plus DECRYPT_OK and PAYLOAD_OK; DECRYPT_NO_SECRET, DECRYPT_CLASS_KEY_EMPTY, KDF_FAIL, AES_DEC_FAIL, MANIFEST_ERR= and HMAC/SHA reject markers absent. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-PL-030`

|`sep_firmware_encrypted_boot_test` |oca_encrypted_boot.bin with sep_efuse_class_key.toml (CLASS_KEY 00..1f asserted). |`sep_boot_measurement_golden_test` |sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml (PROD, SBOOT_DIS=0, clean key fuses), unmodified signed image; golden from primary manifest_hash, lc_state=1, secure_boot=1, sboot_dis=0, demotion_decision=0x2. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim) |Primary boots; MEAS_BOOT_STATE_OK, BL0S_BOOT_PCR=, BL0S_OK printed and the PCR equals the golden; MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000 never printed. |`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |`SEP-ROM-ATT-030`, `SEP-ROM-ATT-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`

DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim) |Primary accepted in order OCA_BODY= .. BL1_JUMP=; one SPI read is exactly the body, no read falls outside body or payload, backup never read; boots BL1. |`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE`, `CHK-STIMULUS-PRIMARY-VERSION`, `CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED`, `CHK-LENGTH-RULE` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-030`

[[sep-rom-vplan-secondary-chiplet]]
===== Recovery and secondary-chiplet manifest path

*Tests:* 5.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_secondary_chiplet_base.py`, for `sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_test`, `sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test`, `sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_invalid_test` and `sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_primary_manifest_invalid_test`. `sep_boot_recovery_test` runs the <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> procedure with the recovery strap set and is judged by `CHK-ROM-PATH`, `CHK-VERDICT` and the checkers in its row.

*Objective:* With STRAPS_LO[25] clear, the ROM takes the secondary-chiplet arm, reads the SMC-published scratch[8] offset and makes exactly one manifest attempt from SMC SRAM, or none when the offset is the invalid marker 0xFFFFFFFF.

*Procedure:*

. Cross-check +sep_smc_scratch8 against the member's manifest_offset (absent for the default 0x1000).
. Parse +sep_smc_mem_hex (based at 0x40060000), assert OCAC at the member's declared image_manifest_offset, and cross-check whether that is the published offset unless the published offset is not an image address.
. Write a TEST_DEV zero eFuse image (the SMC image is unsigned); no +sep_boot_from_spi, so all straps are 0.
. Release the CPU at the Boot ROM base; poll cold_scratch[0]/[1] and the console with a 2M-cycle stall detector and an 8M-cycle limit.
. Assert required markers (STRAPS_LO=0x00000000, STRAP primary=0, recovery=0, BOOT_SECONDARY, MANIFEST_OFF=, SMC_MANIFEST_ADDR=, WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, MANIFEST_PRIMARY, MANIFEST_SRC=) and forbid BOOT_SPI, BOOT_RECOVERY, MANIFEST_BACKUP, SMC_COORD_NOT_READY, SMC_MANIFEST_OFF_INVALID and SPI_INIT markers. A member that expects no slot attempt requires only STRAPS_LO=0x00000000, STRAP primary=0, recovery=0, BOOT_SECONDARY, MANIFEST_OFF= and WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, and forbids SMC_MANIFEST_ADDR=, MANIFEST_PRIMARY, MANIFEST_SRC=, MANIFEST_ALIGN_FAIL and MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED instead of SMC_MANIFEST_OFF_INVALID.
. Assert the console order and, when a slot attempt is expected, exactly one MANIFEST_PRIMARY/MANIFEST_SRC attempt.
. Member check_outcome judges boot or terminal; terminal members also require 20k cycles of quiescence after the verdict.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-SMC-IMAGE` |The packed SMC image is based at 0x40060000, holds OCAC at the member's declared image_manifest_offset, and has the expected relationship between that and the published offset, unless the published offset is not an image address.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-OFFSET` |+sep_smc_scratch8 agrees with the member's offset.
|`CHK-SECONDARY-MARKERS` |Every required secondary-path marker appears.
|`CHK-SECONDARY-FORBIDDEN` |No forbidden marker appears.
|`CHK-SECONDARY-ORDER` |Order STRAPS_LO < BOOT_SECONDARY < MANIFEST_OFF= < SMC_MANIFEST_ADDR= < WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST < MANIFEST_PRIMARY < MANIFEST_SRC=, or STRAPS_LO < BOOT_SECONDARY < MANIFEST_OFF= < WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST when no slot attempt is expected.
|`CHK-SINGLE-ATTEMPT` |When a slot attempt is expected, MANIFEST_PRIMARY and the MANIFEST_SRC= line each appear exactly once.
|`CHK-HANG` |On a non-boot member cold_scratch[1] and the console do not move for 20k cycles after the verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts retired>0, a non-empty console, a non-empty required set, that the SMC image really holds (or lacks) OCAC at the published offset, and that a non-boot member declares expected_error.

*Observation:* ROM console markers and order, cold_scratch[0] verdict, cold_scratch[1] status sequence, SMC image bytes, post-verdict quiescence.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_boot_recovery_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. +sep_straps_lo=00080000 (boot_recovery STRAPS_LO[19]) combined with +sep_boot_from_spi gives STRAPS_LO=0x02080000; +sep_smc_mem_hex staged; flash left bootable. |BOOT_RECOVERY arm: WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, MANIFEST_SRC=0x40061000, MANIFEST_OK and BL1 FUSE_CHK, PASS verdict; BOOT_SPI, BOOT_SECONDARY, SPI_INIT markers absent and zero flash reads. |`CHK-STIMULUS-RECOVERY`, `CHK-RECOVERY-NO-SPI`, `CHK-RECOVERY-ORDER`, `CHK-ROM-PATH`, `CHK-VERDICT` |`SEP-ROM-STRAP-010`
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_test` |Default scratch[8] offset 0x1000 (no +sep_smc_scratch8), unsigned oca_smc_mem.hex, TEST_DEV OTP, straps all 0. |one attempt at 0x40061000 accepted: OCA_BODY=, MFST_VER=, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, SBOOT_OFF, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=, BL1 FUSE_CHK in order; cold_scratch[0] PASS; no PUBK_\*/RSA_EXEC/MANIFEST_ERR=. |`CHK-SECONDARY-BOOT` |`SEP-ROM-STRAP-010`, `SEP-ROM-SMC-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test` |oca_smc_mem_misaligned.hex prepends four bytes to the complete valid image, placing its OCAC manifest and payload bundle at offset 0x1004; +sep_smc_scratch8=00001004 publishes that address; one-shot SLVERR armed on its containing AXI beat at 0x40061000. |one attempt at 0x40061004 refused with MANIFEST_ALIGN_FAIL and MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030108 before OCA_BODY= or any downstream marker; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FAIL verdict and halt. |`CHK-SMC-IMAGE`, `CHK-STIMULUS-OFFSET`, `CHK-STIMULUS-MANIFEST-READ-FAULT`, `CHK-MANIFEST-NO-READ`, `CHK-MANIFEST-SOURCE-ALIGN` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-070`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_invalid_test` |+sep_smc_scratch8=ffffffff publishes the invalid marker while the TB SMC responder sets MANIFEST_READY; unsigned oca_smc_mem.hex holds OCAC at 0x1000; TEST_DEV OTP; straps all 0. |READY is present and scratch[8] is 0xFFFFFFFF: SMC_MANIFEST_OFF_INVALID, WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, MANIFEST_OFFSET_INVALID and MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030109 (OCA_BOOT_ERR_MANIFEST_OFFSET_INVALID) in order; no manifest slot or SMC SRAM source is attempted (no MANIFEST_PRIMARY, MANIFEST_SRC=, MANIFEST_ALIGN_FAIL, MANIFEST_ERR= or MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED); cold_scratch[1] ERROR SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED; FAIL verdict; halt. |`CHK-SMC-IMAGE`, `CHK-STIMULUS-OFFSET`, `CHK-MANIFEST-INVALID-OFFSET` |`SEP-ROM-SMC-050`
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_primary_manifest_invalid_test` |+sep_smc_scratch8=00005000 (offset 0x5000 holds no OCAC in oca_smc_mem.hex); TEST_DEV OTP; straps all 0. |one attempt at 0x40065000 refused MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002, then MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030002; cold_scratch[1] ERROR word; FAIL verdict; halt. |`CHK-INVALID-PUBLISHED-MANIFEST` |`SEP-ROM-SMC-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|===

*Added checker contracts:*

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_boot_recovery_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-RECOVERY` |The driven strap word 0x02080000 sets boot_recovery (STRAPS_LO bit 19).
|`sep_boot_recovery_test` |`CHK-RECOVERY-NO-SPI` |The flash model served zero read transactions.
|`sep_boot_recovery_test` |`CHK-RECOVERY-ORDER` |Console order is STRAPS_LO echo < BOOT_RECOVERY < WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST < SMC MANIFEST_SRC < MANIFEST_OK.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_test` |`CHK-SECONDARY-BOOT` |Single accepted attempt at the published address in the required order, MANIFEST_OK < BL1_COPIED < BL1_JUMP= < FUSE_CHK, and fw_done with PASS.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-MANIFEST-READ-FAULT` |A one-shot SLVERR is armed on the 8-byte AXI beat containing the misaligned manifest source.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test` |`CHK-MANIFEST-NO-READ` |With the one-shot SLVERR armed on the containing beat, observing OCA_BOOT_ERR_MANIFEST_ALIGNMENT instead of OCA_BOOT_ERR_DMA proves the check ran before the first source read.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_misaligned_test` |`CHK-MANIFEST-SOURCE-ALIGN` |The packed image proves OCAC is present at the published 0x1004 offset; exactly one attempt reports MANIFEST_ALIGN_FAIL then OCA_BOOT_ERR_MANIFEST_ALIGNMENT before OCA_BODY=, followed by the generic terminal status, FAIL verdict and quiescence.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_manifest_offset_invalid_test` |`CHK-MANIFEST-INVALID-OFFSET` |With READY set and scratch[8]=0xFFFFFFFF, the console orders STRAPS_LO < BOOT_SECONDARY < MANIFEST_OFF=0xffffffff < SMC_MANIFEST_OFF_INVALID < WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST < MANIFEST_OFFSET_INVALID < MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030109 with the last two once each, no slot or source marker appears, cold_scratch[1] holds the ERROR SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED word, and the verdict is FAIL. The distinct error code and the absent MANIFEST_SRC= prove rom_manifest_boot() refused the marker before forming an address.
|`sep_firmware_secondary_chiplet_bootcode_primary_manifest_invalid_test` |`CHK-INVALID-PUBLISHED-MANIFEST` |One attempt at the published address refused BAD_MAGIC with no body or downstream markers, MANIFEST_ERR= once, ordered src < err < ALL_FAILED < BOOT_FAIL, mapped ERROR word in cold_scratch[1], FAIL verdict.
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-spi-slot-detect]]
===== SPI slot detection and address failover

*Tests:* 5.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test.py`, for `sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` and `sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test`. `sep_spi_detect_success_test` and `sep_rotate_update_set_test` run the <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> procedure, and `sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` runs its own procedure on the common bring-up; each of these three is judged by the checkers in its row.

*Objective:* Proves an erased (blank) primary SPI slot is refused with BAD_MAGIC and the ROM fails over by address to the backup slot and boots it.

*Procedure:*

. Use the default TEST_DEV zero OTP image and the non-secure flash image oca_non_secure_boot.bin.
. Erase the whole primary slot span to 0xFF (payload included) and verify the backup layout is intact.
. Start esrc noise and the console decoder, serve the image over OcahSpiFlash with +sep_boot_from_spi and boot to BL1 PASS on cold_scratch[0].
. Require BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, SPI_INIT_OK, the BAD_MAGIC error and MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000; forbid WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, SPI_INIT_ERR= and the SPI-init-failure skip message.
. Judge console order primary SRC < BAD_MAGIC < backup SRC < MANIFEST_OK.
. Judge the flash record: the primary read returned all 0xFF, the backup read returned OCAC magic, and the primary read precedes the backup read.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SPI` |Primary span fully erased and backup layout verified.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required console marker observed (inherited).
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ORDER` |Console order primary SRC < BAD_MAGIC < backup SRC < MANIFEST_OK.
|`CHK-ADDR-FAILOVER` |Device returned blank bytes for the whole primary read, OCAC magic at 0x41000, primary read first.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |BL1 completion with PASS via cold_scratch[0] (inherited).
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Device-side record must show the primary address was read and returned all 0xFF, and the SPI-init-failure route to the backup is forbidden.

*Observation:* Virt-console markers, OcahSpiFlash transactions (address order, returned bytes), cold_scratch[0] verdict via the boot scoreboard.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_spi_detect_success_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. Unmodified non-secure image, default TEST_DEV; adds SPI_INIT_OK as required and forbids MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000, any MANIFEST_ERR=, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, SPI_INIT_ERR= and the SPI-init-failed skip line. |boots from primary 0x1000; device returned OCAC there and served no read inside the backup span. |`CHK-DETECT-PRIMARY`, `CHK-NO-FAILOVER`, `CHK-ROM-PATH`, `CHK-VERDICT` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-SPI-040`
|`sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. Primary slot's whole flash span (manifest and payload) erased to 0xFF via mm.erase_slot; backup layout verified intact. |primary refused with OCA_FAIL_MAGIC (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002); backup at 0x41000 boots to BL1; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and the SPI-init-failure route absent. |`CHK-STIMULUS-SPI`, `CHK-FAILOVER-ORDER`, `CHK-ADDR-FAILOVER` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |Pre-boot VPI fill of all 32768 SEP SRAM words with a unique non-zero poison; primary slot erased to 0xFF as in the parent. DV shortcut: VPI backdoor write and read of the SEP SRAM macro array (u_sep_sram...u_mem.mem) via sep_public_scope.vlt public_flat_rw; Verilator only |Word 0 holds the erased primary residue, then all words read 0 after MANIFEST_ERR (BAD_MAGIC) and before MANIFEST_BACKUP/backup SRC, then the backup fetch re-dirties word 0; boots to BL1 PASS. |`CHK-SRAM-HANDLE`, `CHK-SRAM-POISON`, `CHK-SRAM-RESIDUE`, `CHK-SRAM-CLEARED`, `CHK-CLEAR-WINDOW`, `CHK-CLEAR-BRACKET` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-040`
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |Both primary and backup slot spans erased to 0xFF in oca_non_secure_boot.bin; +sep_boot_from_spi; TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |terminal: MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002 (OCA_FAIL_MAGIC) twice, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, cold_scratch[1] 0x0F010006 and last non-ring status 0x0F010213, FAIL verdict, no boot. |`CHK-STIMULUS-SPI`, `CHK-CONTROLLER-UP`, `CHK-BOTH-ADDRESSES`, `CHK-TERMINAL`, `CHK-HANG-HELD`, `CHK-NO-BOOT`, `CHK-DEVICE-BLANK` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|`sep_rotate_update_set_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. +sep_straps_hi=04000000 (rotate_update STRAPS_HI[26]); primary slot erased to 0xFF so an unrotated ROM would also boot via failover. |first and only slot read is 0x41000 (MANIFEST_BACKUP, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000) and it boots; MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_PRIMARY and any MANIFEST_ERR= absent; no read in the primary span. |`CHK-STIMULUS-ROTATE`, `CHK-ROTATE-ADDR`, `CHK-ROM-PATH`, `CHK-VERDICT` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`
|===

*Added checker contracts:*

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_spi_detect_success_test` |`CHK-DETECT-PRIMARY` |A flash read covered 0x1000 and the device returned the OCAC magic there.
|`sep_spi_detect_success_test` |`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |No flash read landed inside the backup slot span.
|`sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-SPI` |Primary span is fully erased and the backup layout is intact before load.
|`sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` |`CHK-FAILOVER-ORDER` |Console order is primary MANIFEST_SRC < BAD_MAGIC error < backup MANIFEST_SRC < MANIFEST_OK.
|`sep_spi_primary_fail_backup_test` |`CHK-ADDR-FAILOVER` |The device's primary read returned all 0xFF, its backup read returned OCAC, and the primary read came first.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-SRAM-HANDLE` |The SEP SRAM storage array resolves from cocotb.top through the sep_public_scope.vlt public scope.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-SRAM-POISON` |Every SRAM word reads back its poison before boot.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-SRAM-RESIDUE` |Word 0 changed and settled at the erased-slot pattern while the last word still held poison.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-SRAM-CLEARED` |When the last word reads 0, a full scan finds zero non-zero words.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-CLEAR-WINDOW` |At clear completion the primary BAD_MAGIC line was printed and MANIFEST_BACKUP/backup SRC were not.
|`sep_failover_sram_clear_assertion_test` |`CHK-CLEAR-BRACKET` |Word 0 became non-zero again after the clear, and residue < cleared < redirtied in time.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-SPI` |Both slots are fully erased before preload.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-CONTROLLER-UP` |SPI_INIT_OK present, init-failure paths absent.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-BOTH-ADDRESSES` |primary then backup MANIFEST_SRC= and exactly two BAD_MAGIC errors.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-TERMINAL` |MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED follows the backup attempt, both status words are present and fw_pass=0.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-HANG-HELD` |last non-ring-invalid status is 0x0F010213 after 20k cycles.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-NO-BOOT` |PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= absent.
|`sep_spi_not_detected_terminal_test` |`CHK-DEVICE-BLANK` |SPI reads covering both slot addresses returned all 0xFF, primary first.
|`sep_rotate_update_set_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-ROTATE` |Primary span erased, backup intact and the strap constant sets bit 26.
|`sep_rotate_update_set_test` |`CHK-ROTATE-ADDR` |The first slot read lands in the backup span, no read touches the primary span, and 0x41000 returned OCAC.
|===

==== Memory clears
[[sep-rom-vplan-memory-clear]]
===== Memory clears and zero-fill faults

*Tests:* 5 entries from 2 leaf modules.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test.py`. Each member runs the <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> procedure with the ROM build, markers and stimulus of its row. The four `sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_*_test` entries run `sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_test.py`, and `+zero_fill_fault` selects the clear site.

*Objective:* On the clear-enabled ROM (`boot_rom_clear`, `build_clear/`: `SRAM_SCRUB_BYTES=0x40000`, `ROM_ICCM_CLEAR_FULL=1`) the [S15] SEP SRAM scrub and the [S16] ICCM clear leave the memory they cover at zero. At each place the ROM clears memory with a secure DMA zero fill, one failed fill stops the boot at that place.

*Procedure:*

. Run the <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>> procedure: TEST_DEV zero eFuse image, +sep_boot_from_spi, oca_non_secure_boot.bin in OcahSpiFlash, CPU release at the Boot ROM base and a poll of cold_scratch[0] up to 24M cycles.
. `sep_rom_clear_variant_test`: before reset, write a non-zero poison to every SEP SRAM word and every ICCM row through the Verilator public scope (`env/sep_mem_backdoor.py`) and read back the last word of each. Scan all of SEP SRAM when SRAM_CLR_OK reaches the console, and all of ICCM outside [COPY_DST, COPY_DST + COPY_LEN) when BL1_COPIED does.
. Fault members: set `expect_fw_pass` to False and, after bring-up, drive dma_fill_fault_sel_i (1 = ICCM, 2 = SEP SRAM). While a zero fill whose destination lies in the selected region runs, `tb/tb_top.sv` forces an error onto the DMA host-path write responses, so the fill ends in BUS_ERROR. Every other transfer runs unfaulted. Record each change of cold_scratch[1] on every clk edge.
. Assert the required markers of the member, then the forbidden markers, then the member checks.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required console marker of the member appears, with one PASS record per marker. The forbidden markers of the member (always including WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST) are asserted absent, with no record of their own.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` only: cold_scratch[0] carries the BL1 PASS verdict. The fault members log no CHK-VERDICT PASS: the scoreboard fails them on a PASS verdict and does not require the FAIL verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* `sep_rom_clear_variant_test` asserts that the poison stuck before the boot, that both scans ran, and that at least one ICCM word lies outside the BL1 span. The fault leaf requires dma_fill_fault_count_o to be non-zero, so a case whose clear site never ran a fill fails. The first marker of each order (BL1_DST=ICCM, MANIFEST_ERR=, SRAM_SCRUB_LEN=, SRAM_CLR_OK) shows that the ROM reached the step before the clear site.

*Observation:* ROM virt-console markers and their order, cold_scratch[0] verdict, every cold_scratch[1] value, dma_fill_fault_count_o, and the SEP SRAM and ICCM arrays through sep_public_scope.vlt (`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` only).

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` |+sep_boot_rom_hex selects build_clear/boot_rom.vmem (`boot_rom_clear`); unmodified non-secure image over SPI; nothing preloaded into SEP SRAM for the boot. Requires SRAM_CLR_OK, ICCM_CLR_FULL and BL1_COPIED on top of the base markers and forbids ICCM_PAD=. DV shortcut: VPI backdoor write of the poison to every SEP SRAM word and ICCM row before reset, and backdoor reads of both arrays, via sep_public_scope.vlt; Verilator only. |boots to BL1 PASS; all SEP SRAM words read zero at SRAM_CLR_OK; every ICCM data word outside the BL1 copy span reads zero at BL1_COPIED; ICCM_PAD= absent. |`CHK-SRAM-SCRUBBED`, `CHK-ICCM-CLEARED` |`SEP-ROM-CPU-100`
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_pad_test` |Default build/boot_rom.vmem; unmodified non-secure image; +zero_fill_fault=pad drives dma_fill_fault_sel_i=1 (ICCM). DV shortcut: tb_top forces an error onto the DMA host-path write responses of a zero fill into ICCM. |BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK; BL1_DST=ICCM then ICCM_PAD_FAIL; ICCM_PAD=, BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) and ERROR SEP_MSG_BL1_BAD_ADDR (0x0F010217) on cold_scratch[1]; no PASS verdict. |`CHK-FAULT-FIRED`, `CHK-MARKER-ORDER`, `CHK-STATUS` |None: the [S16] stage text states it (see the contracts)
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_retry_test` |Default build/boot_rom.vmem; primary slot erased to 0xFF (mm.erase_slot, checked erased); +zero_fill_fault=retry drives dma_fill_fault_sel_i=2 (SEP SRAM). DV shortcut: as the pad entry, for a zero fill into SEP SRAM. |BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000; MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002 (OCA_FAIL_MAGIC), SRAM_RETRY_CLR_FAIL, MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030100 (OCA_BOOT_ERR_DMA) in order; MANIFEST_BACKUP, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and MANIFEST_OK absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) and ERROR SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED (0x0F010213) on cold_scratch[1]; no PASS verdict. |`CHK-FAULT-FIRED`, `CHK-MARKER-ORDER`, `CHK-STATUS` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-040`
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_scrub_test` |build_clear/boot_rom.vmem (`boot_rom_clear`); unmodified non-secure image; +zero_fill_fault=scrub drives dma_fill_fault_sel_i=2 (SEP SRAM). DV shortcut: as the retry entry. |SRAM_SCRUB_LEN= then SRAM_CLR_FAIL; SRAM_CLR_OK and MANIFEST_SRC= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) on cold_scratch[1]; no PASS verdict. |`CHK-FAULT-FIRED`, `CHK-MARKER-ORDER`, `CHK-STATUS` |None: the [S15] stage text states it (see the contracts)
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_iccm_clear_test` |build_clear/boot_rom.vmem (`boot_rom_clear`); unmodified non-secure image; +zero_fill_fault=iccm_clear drives dma_fill_fault_sel_i=1 (ICCM). DV shortcut: as the pad entry. |SRAM_CLR_OK then ICCM_CLR_FAIL; ICCM_CLR_FULL and MANIFEST_SRC= absent; WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) on cold_scratch[1]; no PASS verdict. |`CHK-FAULT-FIRED`, `CHK-MARKER-ORDER`, `CHK-STATUS` |None: the [S16] stage text states it (see the contracts)
|===

*Added checker contracts:* each contract states what the check grades and the `rom.adoc` sentence it grades. The console tokens are ROM source names; `rom.adoc` defines only SRAM_RETRY_CLR_FAIL. No fault member grades the FAIL verdict, `bl0_state.error_code` or the halt, beyond the forbidden markers and the absent PASS verdict.

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` |`CHK-ROM-PATH` |SRAM_CLR_OK, ICCM_CLR_FULL and BL1_COPIED appear on top of the base markers, and ICCM_PAD= does not. Spec ([S16]): "`ROM_ICCM_CLEAR_FULL=1` zeroes all of ICCM here instead".
|`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` |`CHK-SRAM-SCRUBBED` |All SEP SRAM words, each poisoned non-zero before reset, read zero when SRAM_CLR_OK reaches the console. Spec ([S15]): "The first `SRAM_SCRUB_BYTES` of SEP SRAM (`0x10000000`, 256 KiB) zeroed with one secure DMA zero fill", with `SRAM_SCRUB_BYTES=0x40000`; `SEP-ROM-CPU-100`: "The ROM shall clear SEP SRAM and ICCM before loading any external content". The number of fills, the ROM's read-back and the order against the first manifest read are not graded (`sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test` grades that order).
|`sep_rom_clear_variant_test` |`CHK-ICCM-CLEARED` |Every ICCM data word outside [COPY_DST, COPY_DST + COPY_LEN), each poisoned non-zero before reset, reads zero when BL1_COPIED reaches the console, and at least one word is checked. Spec ([S16]): "`ROM_ICCM_CLEAR_FULL=1` zeroes all of ICCM here instead (base and size are build-time parameters), clearing power-up residue too"; `SEP-ROM-CPU-100` as above. The ECC bits and the order against the first manifest read are not graded.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_*_test` (4 entries) |`CHK-FAULT-FIRED` |dma_fill_fault_count_o is non-zero at the end of the run: a zero fill with its destination in the selected region ran while the fault was armed. Stimulus control; no `rom.adoc` sentence.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_pad_test` |`CHK-MARKER-ORDER` |BL1_DST=ICCM, then ICCM_PAD_FAIL; with `CHK-ROM-PATH`, the base SPI-path markers appear and ICCM_PAD=, BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP= do not. Spec ([S16]): "a failed pad stops the hand-off."
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_pad_test` |`CHK-STATUS` |cold_scratch[1] takes WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) and ERROR SEP_MSG_BL1_BAD_ADDR (0x0F010217) during the run; their order is not graded. Spec (status table, "any secure-DMA copy or fill"): "`DMA_ERROR` (WARN, the engine reported an error)" and "then the caller's error"; `tbl:oca_boot_err`, `OCA_BOOT_ERR_BL1_BAD_ADDR`: "BL1 load or entry address outside the permitted regions, or the DMA copy of BL1 or its ICCM pad failed."
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_retry_test` |`CHK-MARKER-ORDER` |MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030002 for the erased primary, then SRAM_RETRY_CLR_FAIL, then MANIFEST_BOOT_FAIL=0x00030100; with `CHK-ROM-PATH`, BOOT_SPI and MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000 appear and MANIFEST_BACKUP, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and MANIFEST_OK do not. Spec (`SEP-ROM-RETRY-040` text): "If it fails the ROM prints `SRAM_RETRY_CLR_FAIL` and stages no further slot: the search ends with `OCA_BOOT_ERR_DMA` through the same failure path as exhausting every slot". The absent MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED comes from the ROM source, which prints it only after the last slot fails; no `rom.adoc` sentence. "and the boot halts" is not graded.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_retry_test` |`CHK-STATUS` |cold_scratch[1] takes WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) and ERROR SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED (0x0F010213) during the run. Spec (`SEP-ROM-RETRY-040` text): "which reports `MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED`"; status table: "`DMA_ERROR` (WARN, the engine reported an error)".
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_scrub_test` |`CHK-MARKER-ORDER` |SRAM_SCRUB_LEN=, then SRAM_CLR_FAIL; with `CHK-ROM-PATH`, SRAM_CLR_OK and MANIFEST_SRC= do not appear. Spec ([S15]): "a failure halts the boot." The halt is graded only as no manifest source line and no PASS verdict for the rest of the run.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_scrub_test` |`CHK-STATUS` |cold_scratch[1] takes WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) during the run. Spec: "`DMA_ERROR` (WARN, the engine reported an error)". No ERROR word is graded.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_iccm_clear_test` |`CHK-MARKER-ORDER` |SRAM_CLR_OK, then ICCM_CLR_FAIL; with `CHK-ROM-PATH`, ICCM_CLR_FULL and MANIFEST_SRC= do not appear. Spec ([S16]): "a failed full clear halts the boot". The halt is graded as in the scrub entry.
|`sep_rom_zero_fill_fault_iccm_clear_test` |`CHK-STATUS` |cold_scratch[1] takes WARN SEP_MSG_DMA_ERROR (0x0801009D) during the run. Spec: as the scrub entry. No ERROR word is graded.

==== CPU entry, warm reset and DFT gate

[[sep-rom-vplan-warm-reset]]
===== Warm-reset dispatch

*Tests:* 5.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_warm_dispatch_base.py`, for `sep_warm_reset_below_range_hang_test`, `sep_warm_reset_unarmed_cold_boot_test` and `sep_warm_reset_bad_target_exception_test`. `sep_scratch_7_test` and `sep_warm_reset_invalid_hang_test` run the same seed, stage and sample steps in their own procedure and are judged by the checkers in their rows.

*Objective:* Provides the bring-up and sampling scaffold that seeds cold_scratch[7] and observes which vector.S warm-dispatch leg (cold boot, reject-hang, accept-jump) the ROM takes before C.

*Procedure:*

. Assert +sep_cold_scratch7 is absent (unarmed member) or equals the member's seed and differs from the 0xFFFFFFFF cold poison.
. Write a zero eFuse image stamped TEST_DEV.
. Stage ROM .itcm/.dtcm through tcm_load_i unless stage_tcm is off.
. Release the core at the Boot ROM base.
. Sample cold_scratch[1], cold_scratch[7] and cold_scratch[0] every cycle, recording changes, until a stop status or max_run_cycles.
. Optionally watch 2000 more cycles and record retired PCs to measure the spin span.
. The member judges the status/cold7/verdict sequences and the console.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-HANDLER` |The cold_scratch[7] plusarg matches the member's seed (or is absent for the unarmed leg).
|`CHK-HANG` |On a hang member, assert_hung: cold_scratch[1] holds the resting status, the core retires, and the PC spans at most 64 bytes for 2000 cycles.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Members assert instructions retired and that cold_scratch[7] actually held the seed (or 0) in the sampled sequence.

*Observation:* cold_scratch[1] status sequence, cold_scratch[7] sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict sequence, retired PC span, and console emptiness.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_scratch_7_test` |+sep_cold_scratch7=C0000100 and +sep_iccm_word=C0000100:0000006f (`j .` handler in ICCM); TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: +sep_cold_scratch7 deposit and +sep_iccm_word ICCM poke with ECC; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |warm jump: cold_scratch[1]=0x01010068 (WARM_RESET_JUMP), cold_scratch[7] 0xC0000100->0x0, instruction retired at 0xC0000100, no console. |`CHK-COLD7-SEED`, `CHK-WARM-ANNOUNCE`, `CHK-WARM-JUMP`, `CHK-WARM-POISONED`, `CHK-WARM-NO-COLD` |`SEP-ROM-CPU-110`, `SEP-ROM-CPU-120`, `SEP-ROM-CPU-130`
|`sep_warm_reset_invalid_hang_test` |+sep_cold_scratch7=C0040000 (ICCM base + size, the smallest out-of-range address); TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: +sep_cold_scratch7 deposit; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |terminal hang: cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010069 (WARM_RESET_HANG), no 0x01010068, no cold-boot words or poison, silent console, PC spin <=64 B. |`CHK-STIMULUS-HANDLER`, `CHK-SEED`, `CHK-WARM-REJECT`, `CHK-NO-JUMP`, `CHK-NO-COLD-FALLTHROUGH`, `CHK-PRE-C`, `CHK-HANG` |`SEP-ROM-CPU-120`, `SEP-ROM-CPU-140`
|`sep_warm_reset_below_range_hang_test` |+sep_cold_scratch7=BFFFFFFC (SEP_ICCM_MEM_BASE-4). DV shortcut: +sep_cold_scratch7 testbench deposit; tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010069 (WARM_RESET_HANG); no 0x01010068 jump, no BOOTROM_START/PRESTART_DONE, no 0xFFFFFFFF poison, silent console; PC spins within 64 B. |`CHK-SEED`, `CHK-WARM-REJECT-LOW`, `CHK-NO-JUMP`, `CHK-NO-COLD-FALLTHROUGH`, `CHK-PRE-C`, `CHK-HANG` |`SEP-ROM-CPU-120`, `SEP-ROM-CPU-140`
|`sep_warm_reset_unarmed_cold_boot_test` |No +sep_cold_scratch7, so cold_scratch[7] stays at reset value 0. DV shortcut: tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |cold_scratch[7] 0 -> 0xFFFFFFFF; cold_scratch[1] shows 0x80010044 then 0x80010056; no WARM_RESET_JUMP or WARM_RESET_HANG. |`CHK-UNARMED`, `CHK-POISON`, `CHK-COLD-PROGRESS`, `CHK-NO-WARM-DECISION` |`SEP-ROM-CPU-110`

[[sep-rom-vplan-dft-gate]]
===== Memory-repair and MBIST gate

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_firmware_mbist_fail_test.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that the pre-C DFT gate in vector.S blocks boot on an injected memory-repair or MBIST failure: it publishes the raw status to SMC scratch[10], emits MBIST_FAIL WARN, writes ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED and spins. It lets a status with mem_repair_success, mbist_done and mbist_pass set boot, and still halts when reserved STATUS_RPT bit 2 is blown, because no eFuse lets a failed check continue.

*Procedure:*

. Assert +sep_dft_status is set and equals dft_status_injected, then run the member's stimulus-shape check.
. Write a zero eFuse image stamped TEST_DEV (reserved STATUS_RPT bit 2 unblown; `sep_mbist_fail_skip_fuse_ignored_test` blows it).
. Stage ROM .itcm/.dtcm through tcm_load_i and release the core at the Boot ROM base.
. Sample cold_scratch[1] and SMC scratch[10] each cycle until 0x0F01D001 or 400k cycles.
. Watch 2000 more cycles, recording retired PCs and any change of cold_scratch[1].
. Judge scratch[10], the WARN and terminal status words, console silence, and the PC spin span.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-DFT-READ` |SMC scratch[10] held the injected DFT status word.
|`CHK-DFT-DETECT` |cold_scratch[1] held 0x08010219 (WARN + SEP_MSG_MBIST_FAIL).
|`CHK-DFT-PRE-C` |None of SMC_MEM_CHK, LC=, DFT_STATUS=, CHIP_ID= appears on the console.
|`CHK-DFT-PUBLISH` |Repeats the CHK-DFT-READ assertion on scratch[10].
|`CHK-DFT-NO-BYPASS` |The testbench eFuse image has STATUS_RPT bit 2 clear.
|`CHK-DFT-TERMINAL` |cold_scratch[1] reached 0x0F01D001, did not move for 2000 cycles, and the retired PC spanned at most 64 bytes.
|`CHK-DFT-NO-PROGRESS` |MANIFEST_OK, BL1_COPIED and PRE_JUMP never appear.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts instructions retired and that SMC scratch[10] captured the injected word, tying the run to the stimulus rather than the testbench default 0x113.

*Observation:* cold_scratch[1] status sequence, SMC scratch[10] samples, console lines, and retired PC span after the terminal status.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_mbist_fail_test` |+sep_dft_status=FFFFFFFD (SMC DFX_CTRL_STATUS with only bit 1 mem_repair_success clear); STATUS_RPT bit 2 unblown; TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: +sep_dft_status injection through the TB SMC responder; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |terminal ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED: cold_scratch[1] WARN 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001, SMC scratch[10]=0xFFFFFFFD, silent console, PC spin <=64 B. |`CHK-DFT-READ`, `CHK-DFT-DETECT`, `CHK-DFT-PRE-C`, `CHK-DFT-PUBLISH`, `CHK-DFT-NO-BYPASS`, `CHK-DFT-TERMINAL`, `CHK-DFT-NO-PROGRESS` |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030`
|`sep_firmware_mbist_only_fail_test` |+sep_dft_status=00000012 (mem_repair_success bit 1 set, mbist_done bit 4 set, mbist_pass bit 8 clear). DV shortcut: +sep_dft_status injects DFX_CTRL_STATUS through the SMC responder model; tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |scratch[10]=0x00000012; cold_scratch[1] 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001 (ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED); silent console; PC spins; no boot. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030`
|`sep_firmware_mbist_pass_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. +sep_dft_status=00000112 (bits 1, 4, 8 only; repair_done and abort bits clear) into the SMC responder's DFX_CTRL_STATUS_SMU word. DV shortcut: SMC responder is a flat-memory model; +sep_dft_status sets the DFX_CTRL_STATUS_SMU word it serves. |boots; cold_scratch[1] shows PRESTART_DONE 0x80010056 and never WARN 0x08010219 or ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED 0x0F01D001; SMC scratch[10] stays 0; SMC_MEM_CHK and CHIP_ID= printed. |`CHK-DFT-PASS-STIMULUS`, `CHK-DFT-INJECTED`, `CHK-DFT-REACHED`, `CHK-DFT-NO-WARN`, `CHK-DFT-NO-PUBLISH` |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030`
|`sep_mbist_fail_skip_fuse_ignored_test` |+sep_dft_status=FFFFFFFD (only bit 1 mem_repair_success clear) plus OTP STATUS_RPT bit 2 blown on a TEST_DEV image. DV shortcut: +sep_dft_status injection through the TB SMC responder; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex before reset. |terminal ROM_ERR_DFT_GATE_BLOCKED as in `sep_firmware_mbist_fail_test`: cold_scratch[1] WARN 0x08010219 then 0x0F01D001 with no later change, SMC scratch[10]=0xFFFFFFFD, PC only in the halt spin, no C-runtime console marker. |`CHK-SKIP-FUSE-BLOWN`, `CHK-DFT-STATUS-STABLE`, `CHK-DFT-HALT-SPIN`; it keeps `CHK-DFT-PUBLISH` and `CHK-DFT-DETECT`, redefines `CHK-DFT-TERMINAL` and `CHK-DFT-NO-PROGRESS` (which absorbs `CHK-DFT-PRE-C`), and does not run `CHK-DFT-READ` or `CHK-DFT-NO-BYPASS` |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030`
|===
