[[sep-rom-vplan-demotion-prod]]
===== Demotion under PROD

*Tests:* 6.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_demotion_prod_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves the non-PROD_END [S25] demotion decision writes DEMOTE_1 (and never DEMOTE_2) per the BL1/BL2 demotion_control inputs of the booted primary manifest under PROD.

*Procedure:*

. Load preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml (LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=1) and assert both values.
. Verify both shipped slots are sealed, then set primary demotion_control from the member's (\_SEL, \_AUTH, \_BL2) bits.
. Clear the primary secure_boot_control (signature_type NO_SIGNATURE) so the fuse disables secure boot; narrow both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states to PROD only and re-seal the backup.
. Start a DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 state/lock probe monitor, serve the image over OcahSpiFlash (+sep_boot_from_spi) and boot to BL1 PASS on cold_scratch[0].
. Judge console: LC=PROD, FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: 1, SBOOT_OFF, the row's demotion tokens once each after MANIFEST_OK and before BL1_COPIED/BL1_JUMP=; other rows' tokens and RSA markers forbidden.
. Judge DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 (demote, lock) probes and transition count, FEAT_CTRL against the lifecycle golden, and BL0S_BOOT_PCR against the Python measurement golden.
. Judge flash transactions: only the primary slot was read and it served the planted demotion/selector/secure_boot bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC raw and SBOOT_DIS equal the expected values.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LC-CONSTRAINT` |Both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states equal PROD-only with the selector bit set and no package/system bits.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Packed primary decodes to the member's (sel, auth, bl2), secure_boot off, NO_SIGNATURE, PROD-only LC, valid manifest hash.
|`CHK-BOOT-CHAIN-ORDER` |Only the primary attempt is accepted and MANIFEST_OK < last demotion token < BL1_COPIED < BL1_JUMP=.
|`CHK-DEMOTION-CONSOLE` |Required demotion tokens and values appear exactly once after MANIFEST_OK; all other demotion tokens absent.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-TRANSITIONS` |Every DEMOTE probe change is recorded with its cycle, and at least one sample is present.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |First sample is reset (0b10,0,0b10,0), change count within bounds, final DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 (demote, lock) equal the expectation.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-FEAT-CTRL` |SEC_DIS clear and FEAT_CTRL equals feat_ctrl_expected(LC, SIP_DIS, SYS_DIS, DEMOTE_1, DEMOTE_2).
|`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |BL0S_BOOT_PCR printed by BL1 equals SHA256(0\^32 \|\| SHA256(manifest_hash, lc, sboot, sboot_dis, demotion bits)).
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |Primary manifest read returned OCAC magic and no read touched the backup span.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |Device served exactly the planted selector_bits, demotion_control and secure_boot_control bytes.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* \__init_subclass__ cross-checks each member's declared outcome against outcome_for(); the stimulus is asserted offline and on the served SPI bytes, and the probe monitor must see the reset sample first.

*Observation:* Virt-console demotion tokens (BL1_DEMOTE=, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=, DEMOTE_LOCKED/NOT_LOCKED, DEMOTE: BL2 deferred ...), lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2}\_probe_o, lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o, lcc_security_disable_probe_o, BL1's BL0S_BOOT_PCR= line, OcahSpiFlash transactions, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_prod_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 1, 0): demotion_control BL1_ENABLE=1, BL1_VALID=0, BL2 pair clear. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, lock non-demoted', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_unauth_flag_0_prod_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 1, 1): BL1_ENABLE=1, BL1_VALID=0, BL2_VALID=BL2_ENABLE=1. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, unlocked', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_NOT_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,0), DEMOTE_2=(0,0); exactly one probe sample for the run. |`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 0, 0): BL1_VALID=1, BL1_ENABLE=0, BL2 pair clear. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; BL1_DEMOTE=0, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_unauth_flag_0_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 1, 1): BL1_VALID=BL1_ENABLE=1 and BL2_VALID=BL2_ENABLE=1. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; BL1_DEMOTE=1, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(1,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`

|`sep_firmware_demotion_bl2_request_prod_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 0, 1): only the BL2 pair set. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, unlocked', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_NOT_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,0), DEMOTE_2=(0,0); exactly one probe sample; boot PCR demotion bits 0x4. |`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`

|`sep_firmware_demotion_bl2_request_prod_test` |`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |Tightened: demote_changes_min = demote_changes_max = 1.

[[sep-rom-vplan-demotion-prod-end]]
===== Demotion under PROD_END

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_demotion_prod_end_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that at PROD_END the ROM ignores every manifest demotion input and writes both DEMOTE_1 and DEMOTE_2 not demoted and locked, on a fully verified secure boot.

*Procedure:*

. Load preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_end.toml (LC raw 0x8, SBOOT_DIS=0) and assert both values.
. Verify both shipped slots are sealed, then set primary demotion_control from the member's (\_SEL, \_AUTH, \_BL2) bits.
. Narrow both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states to PROD_END only and re-seal (re-sign) both slots.
. Start the DEMOTE probe monitor and esrc noise, serve the image over OcahSpiFlash (+sep_boot_from_spi, +esrc_noise_force) and boot to BL1 PASS.
. Judge console: LC=PROD_END, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK, 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock' and DEMOTE_LOCKED once; BL1_DEMOTE=, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC= and SBOOT_OFF absent.
. Judge DEMOTE_1=(0,1) and DEMOTE_2=(0,1), FEAT_CTRL golden, and BL0S_BOOT_PCR with demotion bits 0x2.
. Judge flash transactions: only the primary was read and it served the planted demotion bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Packed primary decodes to the member's (sel, auth, bl2) and PROD_END-only lifecycle_states.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LC-CONSTRAINT` |Both slots' lifecycle_states equal PROD_END-only with the selector bit set.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC raw 0x8 and SBOOT_DIS=0 (inherited).
|`CHK-BOOT-CHAIN-ORDER` |Single primary attempt with PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK and PAYLOAD_OK in order, and MANIFEST_OK before the last demotion token, before BL1_COPIED, before BL1_JUMP= (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTION-CONSOLE` |'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock' and DEMOTE_LOCKED once after MANIFEST_OK; all other demotion tokens absent (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |Final DEMOTE_1=(0,1) and DEMOTE_2=(0,1) from reset (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTE-FEAT-CTRL` |FEAT_CTRL matches the non-demoted PROD_END profile (inherited).
|`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |Boot PCR matches the golden with demotion bits 0x2 and bl2 forced 0 (inherited).
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |No read touched the backup span (inherited).
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |Device served the planted demotion inputs (inherited).
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Planted inputs are asserted on the packed image and on the served SPI bytes, because the PROD_END consoles are identical across members; the DEMOTE_2 lock 0->1 proves the lock_2 probe is live.

*Observation:* Virt-console signed-path and demotion tokens, lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2}\_probe_o, lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o, BL0S_BOOT_PCR=, OcahSpiFlash transactions, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force forces the ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task |Boots; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock', DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`

==== Hardware stall timeouts

[[sep-rom-vplan-stall-timeout]]
===== Secure DMA and HMAC stalls

*Tests:* 2.

*Procedure:* each member is an independent test on the common bring-up. It applies the stimulus in its row, waits for the outcome in its row, and judges it with the checkers in its row.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_dma_stall_terminal_test` |+sep_dma_host_stall holds the DMA host response channel idle; +sep_smc_mem_hex SMC-SRAM manifest path; TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: +sep_dma_host_stall (TB holds the DMA host port response idle). |terminal: DMA_TIMEOUT_STS= then MANIFEST_ERR=OCA_BOOT_ERR_DMA (0x00030100), WARN 0x08010229 before ERROR 0x0F010213, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FAIL verdict. |`CHK-DMA-STALL-TIMEOUT`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-STATUS`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-TERMINAL`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-NO-BOOT` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|`sep_rom_hmac_fifo_stall_terminal_test` |dut.hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i driven to 1 once CRYPTO_SELFTEST_OK appears on the console; TEST_DEV; no SPI flash. DV shortcut: Forced TB input hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i triggered from a console marker; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |terminal ROM_ERR_ROM_HASH_MISMATCH: SHA_FIFO_TIMEOUT then ROM_HASH_COMPUTE_FAIL, WARN 0x0801022A before 0x0F01F005, FAIL verdict, halt. |`CHK-HMAC-STALL-STIMULUS`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-TIMEOUT`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-STATUS`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-TERMINAL`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-NO-PROGRESS` |`SEP-ROM-ATT-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|===

==== Spec-graded ROM invariants

[[sep-rom-vplan-spec-graded]]
===== Leaves graded on `rom.adoc` shall sentences

*Tests:* 40 entries from 26 leaf modules, all in `rom_fw.toml` and in the `rom_fw` group.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_rom_observe_base.py` for the leaves that start from the common bring-up. The other leaves extend an existing test, keep all of its checks and add their own; the Stimulus cell names the parent.

*Objective:* Each leaf grades `rom.adoc` shall sentences that the older groups do not grade. The leaf's module docstring quotes each sentence verbatim and states which sentences or clauses it does not grade. Expected values come from `rom.adoc` and the other ROM documents in `bootrom/prod/doc/`, with two exceptions: the status-ring leaves take the ring header layout from the SMC ROM documents (`hw/sys/smc/bootrom/prod/doc/status-coordination.adoc`) and the SMC DV ring struct (`hw/sys/smc/dv/fw/include/smc_ring_buffer.h`), because the SEP ROM documents give no layout, and the RSA exponent leaf takes the raw public-key layout from the OCA implementation, because `rom.adoc` gives the raw key as a modulus with no exponent. A spec-correct check that fails on the current ROM stays in the testlist, and its entry comment states the spec sentence that the ROM does not meet.

*Procedure:*

. Start `SepRomObserver` (`env/sep_rom_observer.py`) before reset release when the leaf grades order. It records retirements, the `cold_scratch[0]` and `cold_scratch[1]` words, the decoded console, watched ports and fabric beats, each with the time of the `clk_i` edge that showed it.
. Apply the eFuse image, the plusargs and the runtime stimulus of the member row.
. Run until the leaf's stop condition, bounded by its cycle limit. An observer-base leaf that does not reach it fails `CTRL-RUN-REACHED`.
. Grade the record through `SepChkLog` (`env/sep_chk_log.py`). Each `CHK-<NAME>` grades one contract below, each `CTRL-<NAME>` proves that the record a check reads was live, and `OBS-<NAME>` logs a value without grading it. A false control fails the leaf like a failed check.

*Anti-vacuity:* An absence check (no write, no read, no interrupt) needs a control that shows its tap or record saw the healthy activity of the same kind in the same run. A check does not grade absence through `last_store()` or `Retire.mem_addr`, which cannot tell an unresolved address from no access.

*Observation:* `cold_scratch[0]` verdict, `cold_scratch[1]` status words, the scratch console, the CPU retirement trace, the PR-LSU-XBAR, PR-LSU-ROM, PR-SMC and PR-TCM fabric taps, the SRAM and ICCM macro ports, the flash BFM log and the debug-module system-bus reads named in each row.

Where the specification contradicts itself or leaves a value undefined, the leaf grades only the undisputed part and logs the rest as OBS, and the contract below names the clause it does not grade and why. `SEP-ROM-HOFF-050` has no test (<<sep-rom-vplan-known-limitations>>).

*Members:*

|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |Runs `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` (sep_efuse_class_key.toml: TEST_DEV, debug open, SBOOT_DIS 0, golden CLASS_KEY); both slots carry `oca_encrypted_boot.bin` made validly unsigned (`secure_boot_control` 0, signing fields zero, hash resealed). DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force. |Both slots refused, no decryption, terminal halt. |`CHK-ENCRYPTED-REFUSED`, `CHK-REFUSAL-CAUSE`, `CHK-NO-DECRYPTION`, `CHK-TERMINAL` |`SEP-ROM-PL-050`
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_success` |Observer base; SPI boot; sep_efuse_class_key.toml; encrypted primary re-encrypted under an IV that shares no 16-byte window with the key material; backup magic broken; +sep_rom_decrypt_exit=success. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force. |Boots; no key-material residue in DCCM at the stage return and at the hand-off mret. |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE`, `CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE-AT-END` |`SEP-ROM-PL-080`
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_init_fail` |As above with +sep_rom_decrypt_exit=aes_init_fail. DV shortcut: tb input aes_sw_rst_readback_inject_i makes the AES bit of SW_RESET_N read back 0 from the stage entry to its return; the AES itself stays out of reset. |No residue at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict. |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE`, `CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE-AT-END` |`SEP-ROM-PL-080`
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_kdf_fail` |As above with +sep_rom_decrypt_exit=kdf_fail. DV shortcut: hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i stops the HMAC message FIFO from the stage entry to its return. |No residue at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict. |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE`, `CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE-AT-END` |`SEP-ROM-PL-080`
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_dec_fail` |As above with +sep_rom_decrypt_exit=aes_dec_fail. DV shortcut: tb input aes_ctrl_update_err_inject_i forces the AES core's CTRL update error from the stage entry to its return, so the cipher configuration is rejected before the key load. |No residue at the stage return and at the FAIL verdict. |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE`, `CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE-AT-END` |`SEP-ROM-PL-080`
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |Observer base with the SPI boot setup of `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test` (+sep_boot_from_spi, OcahSpiFlash, ESRC noise). DV shortcut: from the first MANIFEST_LOAD_START word to the verdict, tb input dma_host_rsp_err_inject_i makes every secure-DMA host response outside a zero fill SLVERR after the first dma_host_rsp_err_pass_beats_i = 4 R and B beats of each transfer, so the SEP SRAM clear between slots completes and each DMA read lands the frame's first 8 flash bytes, the manifest magic included, in the destination buffer before it fails (CTRL-PARTIAL-LANDED, from the PR-SRAM tap). |2 to 4 reads per slot, backup after primary, one MANIFEST_LOAD_START per slot attempt, the partial buffer not acted on, ERROR MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED and 0xDEADBEEF; halt. |`CHK-READ-RETRIED`, `CHK-ATTEMPTS-BOUNDED`, `CHK-NO-PARTIAL-USE`, `CHK-FALLBACK-OR-TERMINAL`, `CHK-LOAD-START-PER-ATTEMPT` |`SEP-ROM-SPI-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|===

*Added checker contracts:* each contract states what the check grades and the `rom.adoc` sentence it grades.

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-RETIRE` |The observer's retirement count equals the `cpu_trace_mon` count within one, and no trap is recorded.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-KEEP` |The keep filter retains stores, fences and SYSTEM instructions.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-RESET-VECTOR` |The first retirement is at `_start` = 0x10040000, the Boot ROM base. Spec: "The core enters at `_start` in the Boot ROM."
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-STATUS-ENCODE` |More than five pre-mret words carry the STATUS_ENCODE BL0 layout, which proves the cold_scratch[1] lane. Spec: "every `STATUS_OUT()` encoded status word lands here".
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-STORE-LAG` |At least ten status records match a retired cold_scratch[1] store, each 1 to 64 clk after it. A status record whose store address the tracker cannot resolve stays unmatched and is not graded.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-CONSOLE` |MANIFEST_OK precedes the hand-off mret, and the console BL1_JUMP= address equals the first post-mret PC.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-MRET` |The 64-retirement ring ends on mret, and the first post-mret PC equals the entry address the status channel reports. Spec: "`STARTING_BL1` → `INFO_EXT` ×2 (entry address)".
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-PC` |The PC watch records `rom_main`, and no retirement falls outside the watch window.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-WATCH` |The watched run request goes 0, 1, 0 with the pulse width that bring-up drives.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-TRACKER` |Every resolved pmpaddr0 write operand is 0x04011FFF, the Boot ROM entry of the reference PMP region set.
|`sep_rom_observer_smoke_test` |`CHK-OBS-VERDICT` |cold_scratch[0] ends on 0xACAFACA1. Spec: "`0xACAFACA1` PASS ... in DV".
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-HART-GATE` |An mhartid read into a non-zero rd retires before the first data access (load or store). Spec: "Only hart 0 shall execute the boot flow." and "*Hart 0* is the hart whose `mhartid` CSR reads zero."
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-MTVEC-FIRST` |An mtvec write retires before the first load or store. Spec: "The trap vector (`mtvec`) shall be programmed before the first MMIO access". The mtvec value is not graded: the reset-stub figure names `trap_vector`, and `rom.adoc` does not say which vector applies before sp is set.
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-MRAC-FIRST` |An MRAC (0x7C0) write retires before the first load or store, so before the warm-scratch read. Spec: "The VeeR MRAC CSR (`0x7C0`) shall be configured ... before the warm-scratch register is read".
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-ENTRY-ORDER` |The hart read, the mtvec write and the MRAC write retire in that order, all before the first data access. Spec: "The following actions occur strictly in order, before any other memory or MMIO access:"
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-MRAC-VALUE` |The last MRAC write before the first data access is a csrrw of 0xAAAAAAA8. Spec: "(value `0xAAAAAAA8`: side-effect, non-cacheable attributes for device regions)".
|`sep_rom_reset_entry_order_test` |`CHK-HART-NO-PARK` |No retirement falls in the secondary-hart park loop. Spec: "Any other hart shall set its trap vector and park permanently in a `wfi` loop." Only the hart-0 side is reachable on the single-hart core.
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-NO-FILTER-WRITE` |No CPU write to an OUTBOUND_FILTER_CTRL register before the hand-off mret. Spec: "the ROM shall open no outbound window at all." Default-deny and cold_scratch-only reporting are not graded, because other `rom.adoc` sections reach the SMC through an outbound window and mirror status into the SMC SRAM ring.
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-NO-IRQ` |No interrupt trap and no entry at a watched trap vector before the hand-off mret. Spec: "Interrupts shall remain disabled for the entire ROM execution".
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-NO-MIE-SET` |No mie or mstatus write sets an interrupt-enable bit; a write that sets mstatus.MPIE counts. Spec: "no ROM stage re-enables them."
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-MIE-CLEAR-ORDER` |mie and mstatus.MIE are cleared after the "entered `_start`" status store and before the first PMP write. Spec: "`mie` is cleared and `mstatus.MIE` is cleared at entry". `rom.adoc` does not define "at entry", so the clause is read as its place in the cold-boot list.
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-NO-WDT` |No CPU write to a WDT register, and the WDT ports stay at a known 0. Spec: "The ROM shall not arm the watchdog timer."
|`sep_rom_boot_quiet_invariants_test` |`CHK-STACK-GLOBALS-DCCM` |`__stack_top` and `__global_pointer$` lie in DCCM, the ROM loads them into sp and gp, and no resolved sp or gp value leaves DCCM. Spec: "The stack and the C globals shall live in DCCM."
|`sep_rom_dccm_scrub_test` |`CHK-DCCM-WRITE-BEFORE-READ` |Every DCCM read on the PR-TCM port from reset to the hand-off mret hits a word written earlier in the run. Spec: "so that no uninitialized-ECC read and no stale value from a previous boot reaches the boot flow."
|`sep_rom_dccm_scrub_test` |`CHK-DCCM-READ-LAST-WRITE` |Every DCCM read returns the data and ECC of the last write to that word. Spec: as above.
|`sep_rom_dccm_scrub_test` |`CHK-DCCM-SPEC-RANGE-WRITTEN` |Every word in [DCCM base, `__stack_top`) takes a zero write before the first `rom_main` retirement. Spec: "The ROM shall leave DCCM in a deterministic state before the C runtime reads it" and "The stub zero-fills DCCM from its base up to at most `__stack_top`." The upper bound is not graded, because other `rom.adoc` sections and `hardware-initialization.adoc` scrub the full 128 KiB DCCM.
|`sep_rom_dccm_scrub_test` |`CHK-DCCM-PLANTED-NOT-REACHED` |No read returns a word that holds a planted double-bit error (the planted-word subset of READ-LAST-WRITE). Spec: as WRITE-BEFORE-READ.
|`sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test` |`CHK-SRAM-CLEARED-BEFORE-LOAD` |Every SEP SRAM byte takes a zero write on the SRAM request port before the first PR-SMC manifest read beat. Spec: "The ROM shall clear SEP SRAM and ICCM before loading any external content".
|`sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test` |`CHK-SRAM-RESIDUE-CLEARED-AT-LOAD` |The seven probed SRAM words planted through +sep_sram_hex read zero at that beat. Spec: "so that no stale or attacker-resident data can survive into the validated-boot environment."
|`sep_rom_sram_iccm_clear_test` |`CHK-ICCM-CLEARED-BEFORE-LOAD` |Every ICCM bank row takes a zero write on the ICCM macro port before that beat. Spec: as SRAM-CLEARED-BEFORE-LOAD.
|`sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test`, `sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test_hold` |`CHK-NO-FUSE-READ-BEFORE-SENSE` |No CPU read of the SEP eFuse map on PR-LSU-XBAR while smc_fuse_sense_done_i is low. Spec: "The ROM shall not consume any fuse shadow value before fuse sensing has completed."
|`sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test` |`CHK-POLL-GATES-READ` |An SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS poll precedes the first eFuse read and saw sense done. Spec: "Completion is indicated by `SEP_CPU_CTRL_SMC_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` bit 0 (`smc_fuse_sense_done`); the ROM polls it" and "The poll is a discrete stage ([S08]) that runs unconditionally, ahead of every fuse consumer".
|`sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test` |`CHK-SENSE-THEN-READ` |Every eFuse read, LC_STATE included, follows sense completion; the positive control that the tap records the ROM's eFuse reads. Spec: as NO-FUSE-READ-BEFORE-SENSE.
|`sep_rom_fuse_sense_wait_test_hold` |`CHK-HOLD-WAITS` |With sense held not-done, no eFuse read and the poll still running at the end of the 1.3M-cycle window. Spec: "the ROM polls it without timeout", graded up to the window.
|`sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_*` (7 entries) |`CHK-LC-RECORDED` |After the first LC_STATE read, the raw value is written zero-extended to `bl0_state.lc_state` on the PR-TCM DCCM write port, and the word holds it at the hand-off mret or at the FAIL verdict. Spec: "The validated LC state shall be recorded in `bl0_state.lc_state` for consumption by later stages and by BL1". Reads by later stages and BL1 are not graded.
|`sep_rom_lc_state_record_test_*` (7 entries) |`CHK-LC-REPORTED` |cold_scratch[1] carries FUSE_LC_STATE 0x01010020, then INFO_EXT 0x8101000<raw>, then LIFECYCLE_VALID 0x0101002C. Spec: "and shall be reported on the status channel." The ring channel is not graded.
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-PMP-VALUES-KNOWN` |The tracker resolves every PMP and mseccfg write operand of the cold rule set. Precondition of the checks below; it grades no spec sentence.
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-RLB-FIRST` |An mseccfg write that sets RLB retires before the first pmpcfg write that sets an L bit. Spec: "The ROM shall set `mseccfg.RLB=1` *before* programming any locked PMP rule".
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-ALL-LOCKED` |Every active rule of the cold set has L=1. Spec: "and shall program all fence rules with `L=1`." and "the fence entries shall either carry the lock bit (`L=1`) or an equivalent machine-mode-binding mechanism".
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-MMWP-AFTER-RULES` |An mseccfg write that sets MMWP retires after the last rule write. Spec: "After the locked rule set is programmed, the ROM shall set `mseccfg.MMWP=1`".
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-NO-CATCH-ALL` |No active rule covers the whole address space. Spec: "No catch-all entry is used".
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-WINDOWS-MATCH-SPEC` |The windows and permissions of the cold rule set equal tbl:pmp_regions; the XIP windows join only when an XIP driver is linked, because `rom.adoc` makes them conditional in one place and unconditional in another and gives no XIP base or size. Spec: "Data windows shall be limited to the regions the ROM demonstrably needs" and "Everything else shall have no PMP grant."
|`sep_rom_pmp_program_test` |`CHK-ONLY-ROM-EXEC` |Only the Boot ROM window carries execute in the cold rule set. Spec: "instruction fetch shall be possible only from the Boot ROM region (`0x1004_0000`, 64 KiB)" and SEP SRAM and ICCM "shall be writable but *non-executable* (`RW-`)". Fetch traps are not graded.
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-FETCH-ROM-ONLY` |No retirement outside the Boot ROM from the first PMP write to the release. Spec: "instruction fetch shall be possible only from the Boot ROM region".
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-SINGLE-RELEASE` |Between the cold rule set and the hand-off mret, the only rule-set change is one write to the BL1-region rule; an mseccfg write that only clears RLB is logged as OBS-RLB-AT-JUMP. Spec: "shall be implemented as a single rewrite of the locked BL1-region rule" and "shall not modify any other rule at release."
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-RELEASE-ADDS-ONLY-X` |The release adds execute to the BL1-region rule and changes no other bit. Spec: "from `RW-` to executable".
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-RELEASE-UNDER-RLB` |RLB is 1 and MMWP is not cleared at the release. Spec: "legal only while `mseccfg.RLB=1`" and "The ROM shall not clear `mseccfg.MMWP`".
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-RELEASE-AFTER-PROGRESS` |The release follows MANIFEST_VALIDATED, FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED and HANDOFF_CHECK. Spec: "Only after the self-check passes shall the ROM release the PMP execution fence". The "passes" clause is logged as blocked: no spec status reports a passed self-check.
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-RELEASE-LAST-CSR-WRITE` |No CSR write, other than an RLB clear, between the release and the mepc write. Spec: "The release shall be the last write before the jump sequence." Stores after the release are OBS only: `rom.adoc` does not say whether "write" includes stores, and its hand-off figures copy the BL1 image after the release.
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-JUMP-SEQUENCE` |mepc write, fence.i, fence and mret retire in that order before the hand-off. Spec: "`mepc ← entry`, `fence.i` (IFU sees the new SRAM contents), `fence` (stores complete), then `mret`."
|`sep_rom_pmp_release_handoff_test` |`CHK-JUMP-TARGET` |The first post-mret PC equals `load_addr + entry_point` of the TOC BL1 entry and the status-reported entry. Spec: "control transfers to `load_addr + entry_point`".
|`sep_rom_demote_after_lock_order_test` |`CHK-DEMOTE1-AFTER-LOCK` |The first clk edge where the LOCKS shadow word holds all six read-lock bits is strictly earlier than the first edge where DEMOTE_1 leaves its reset value. Spec: "The `DEMOTE_1` write shall be ordered after the fuse-secret read-locks of [S25]/[S26]". The clause "the last fuse state BL0 changes" is OBS only; the second sentence is not graded.
|`sep_rom_version_hash_report_test` |`CHK-CONSOLE-VERSION` |The console carries the BL0_VERSION string of the image's build. Spec: "The ROM shall report its version string and self-hash at every boot ([S05]): the full value through the debug console".
|`sep_rom_version_hash_report_test` |`CHK-CONSOLE-HASH` |The console carries the SHA-256 of [ROM base, `__metadata_end`) of the loaded image. Spec: as CONSOLE-VERSION, with the hashed region from SEP-ROM-ATT-010: "the hash covers the `.text` + `.metadata` bytes — `[ROM base, __metadata_end)`".
|`sep_rom_version_hash_report_test` |`CHK-STATUS-HASH-PREFIX` |On cold_scratch[1], the ROM_HASH word is followed by two INFO_EXT words that carry the first 32 digest bits, most significant half first; `rom.adoc` does not define the order of the two halves. Spec: "a machine-readable prefix (first 16 bits × 2) as `INFO_EXT` status words following `SEP_MSG_ROM_HASH` on the status channel."
|`sep_rom_version_hash_report_test` |`CHK-REPORT-AT-S05` |ROM_HASH and both console lines fall after BOOTROM_START_MAIN and before the [S06] DEVICE_MODE_INPUTS_CHECK word. Spec: "at every boot ([S05])"; one cold boot is graded.
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |`CHK-SEPARATE-SLOTS` |Exactly two enrollments (RECORD_MEASUREMENT and its INFO_EXT slot word), carrying slot 0 and slot 1. Spec: "BL0's two measurements occupy separate slots ... slot 0 = ROM identity, slot 1 = boot configuration/state". The slot-0 value is not graded.
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |`CHK-SLOT0-AFTER-VERIFY` |Every byte of [ROM base, `__metadata_end`) is read on the LSU ROM port before the slot-0 store. Spec (SEP-ROM-ATT-060): "Before enrollment, the ROM shall *verify* its embedded self-hash by recomputing SHA-256 over the hashed ROM region". The read stands for the recompute; the compare is not graded.
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |`CHK-VERIFY-AFTER-S14-S10` |That read starts after all eight HMAC DIGEST words are read following CRYPTO_INIT_CHECK and after the first BL0_STATE_INIT word. Spec (SEP-ROM-ATT-060): "This runs after the crypto self-test ([S14]) and `bl0_state` initialization ([S10])."
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |`CHK-SLOT0-BEFORE-SLOT1` |The slot-0 enrollment precedes the slot-1 enrollment, the order of fig:measurement_compose and of the stages [S17] and [S27].
|`sep_rom_pcrv_slot_order_test` |`CHK-SLOT1-AFTER-VALIDATION` |MANIFEST_VALIDATED, then the slot-1 enrollment, then the hand-off mret. Spec (SEP-ROM-ATT-030): "After the manifest has fully validated ... the ROM shall compose the boot-state record below, enroll it into slot 1". The clause "before the fuse-secret locks" is OBS only, because the boot-flow figure and the stage table place the slot-1 enrollment after the DEMOTE_1 write, which follows the locks.
|`sep_rom_smc_scratch_sanity_test` |`CHK-SANITY-WRITES` |The first four scratch[7] writes carry the four spec patterns, in any order, each with all four byte strobes. Spec: "four patterns (`0xA5A55A5A`, `0x12345678`, `0x00000000`, `0xFFFFFFFF`) are written to scratch[7] and read back."
|`sep_rom_smc_scratch_sanity_test` |`CHK-SANITY-READBACKS` |Each write is read back before the next scratch[7] write, with an OKAY response and equal data. Spec: as SANITY-WRITES.
|`sep_rom_smc_scratch_sanity_test` |`CHK-SANITY-RESTORE` |After the fourth read-back the last scratch[7] write up to the hand-off mret is zero, and the responder holds zero. Spec: "the register shall be restored to zero on success." The 0xA001 mismatch path and "Before any protocol decision depends on the SMC" are not graded: the [S03] gate reads the SMC DFX status and straps before the sanity check, and `rom.adoc` does not say whether those reads are exempt.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_ready_wait_test` |`CHK-NO-CONSUME-BEFORE-READY` |No PR-SMC read of scratch[8] from the wait status to the release, and no SMC SRAM read from the first scratch[9] poll to the release. Spec: "The ROM shall wait (unbounded poll) for `scratch9.MANIFEST_READY` before consuming scratch[8]." The pre-wait probe read of scratch[8] is OBS only, because `rom.adoc` does not define "consuming".
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_ready_wait_test` |`CHK-CONSUMED-PUBLISHED-OFFSET` |After the release: a scratch[9] poll, a scratch[8] read and the manifest fetch from SMC SRAM at the published offset; no read of the decoy page; MANIFEST_SRC= names the published address. Spec: "The manifest is copied by secure DMA from `SMC_SRAM_base + scratch[8]` into SEP SRAM". The clause "by secure DMA" is not graded.
|`sep_rom_smc_manifest_offset_invalid_test_*` (3 entries) |`CHK-SLOT-REFUSED` |Between the post-wait scratch[8] read and the verdict an ERROR MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED; no MANIFEST_VALIDATED; verdict 0xDEADBEEF; no status or console record after it; no MANIFEST_OK or BL1_JUMP= line. Spec: "The ROM shall reject a manifest offset of `0xFFFFFFFF` and shall bounds-check the staged manifest ... against the 1 MiB SMC SRAM window before reading." The clause "before reading" is OBS only, because `smc-coordination.adoc` says the ROM does not range-check the manifest header read and leaves the offset to the SMC; the `_manifest_straddle` entry shows reads before the refusal, which `rom.adoc` alone does not allow.
|`sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_*` (2 entries) |`CHK-RING-NO-WRITE-OUTSIDE` |Up to the hand-off every PR-SMC write lies in the SMC SRAM window or the SMC scratch block; `straddle`: no write touches the header; `bad_header`: every window write lies in the declared ring. Spec: "never write outside the window".
|`sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_*` (2 entries) |`CHK-INVALID-REPORTED` |After the ROM reads scratch[11] (`bad_header`: after its first header read), cold_scratch[1] carries BL0 STATUS_REPORTING_INVALID (code 0x79; the type is logged only, because `rom.adoc` defines ERROR as fatal while this report lets the boot continue). Spec: "report `STATUS_REPORTING_INVALID` on the scratch channel".
|`sep_rom_status_ring_bounds_test_*` (2 entries) |`CHK-BOOT-CONTINUES` |The invalid-ring report precedes MANIFEST_OK, and the boot reaches the BL1 PASS verdict. Spec: "shall degrade gracefully on an invalid ring". The clause "never disable reporting on a possibly-transient error" is not graded: `rom.adoc` does not say whether an invalid scratch[11] offset is a possibly-transient error.
|`sep_rom_status_ring_severity_test` |`CHK-RING-SEVERITY-RESERVE` |At the end of the run `entries[0..k)` equal expected_ring(offered, 8), head is k and the slots past k hold the fill words; offered is every non-DEBUG cold_scratch[1] word after the first scratch[11] read. Spec: "`INFO`-class inserts require 3 free slots and degrade to an `INFO_OVERFLOW` marker at exactly 3; `WARN` requires 2, degrading to `WARNING_OVERFLOW`; `ERROR` inserts down to the last free slot." The ring layout and the retention rule are inferences, because the SEP ROM documents define neither.
|`sep_rom_oca_library_callback_test` |`CHK-TABLE-WIRED` |Each of the eight role members of the ROM's `oca_callbacks_t` table holds a ROM function outside the library text (static check of the loaded image). Spec: "supplying its hardware bindings through the `oca_callbacks_t` table".
|`sep_rom_oca_library_callback_test` |`CHK-LIBRARY-ENTERED` |`oca_validate` or `oca_validate_manifest` retires before the MANIFEST_VALIDATED word. Spec: "The ROM shall validate manifests with the freestanding OCA validation library".
|`sep_rom_oca_library_callback_test` |`CHK-CALLBACKS-FROM-LIBRARY` |`sha256`, `verify_signature`, `get_root_key_revocation` and `get_security_version` are each entered between the last library entry and MANIFEST_VALIDATED by a jump from library text. Spec: "(SHA-256 via the HMAC block, RSA-3072 via OTBN, ... device revocation and security-version state from the efuse map)". The hardware route of each callback is not graded.
|`sep_rom_entropy_health_fail_terminal_test` |`CHK-ENTROPY-FATAL` |ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED follows ENTROPY_INIT_START; the last status word is ERROR ROM_ERR_ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED (0x0F01F009) after it; the last verdict is 0xDEADBEEF. Spec: "A failed bring-up shall stop the boot, reported as `SEP_MSG_ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED` ... and halt on `ROM_ERR_ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED`."
|`sep_rom_entropy_health_fail_terminal_test` |`CHK-STAGE-MARKER` |A console line between ENTROPY_INIT_START and the failure report names the health test. Spec: "with a console marker naming the stage that failed".
|`sep_rom_entropy_health_fail_terminal_test` |`CHK-NO-SLOT-RETRY` |After the failure: no slot console marker, no MANIFEST_LOAD_START or MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED word, no SPI chip-select assertion. Spec: "It shall *not* be treated as a per-slot failure".
|`sep_rom_entropy_health_fail_terminal_test` |`CHK-HALTED` |For 20,000 clk after the verdict the retired PCs span at most 64 bytes and cold_scratch[1], cold_scratch[0] and the console do not change. Spec: "halt on `ROM_ERR_ENTROPY_INIT_FAILED`", with SEP-ROM-RETRY-050 (d) "parks in `wfi`".
|`sep_rom_entropy_lock_order_test` |`CHK-FIPS-LOCK-AFTER-HEALTH` |At least one MAIN_SM_STATUS read in the bring-up window, every one before the first FIPS_LOCK AW, and the last gated-out ESRC sample before that AW. Spec: "The ROM shall lock the ESRC configuration (`FIPS_LOCK`) after the startup health test passes".
|`sep_rom_entropy_lock_order_test` |`CHK-FIPS-LOCK-BEFORE-CONSUMER` |The FIPS_LOCK write is the locking write, and its B precedes the first entropy consumer event (CSRNG seed ack, DRBG stream beat or crypto EDN delivery). Spec: "and *before* any entropy reaches a consumer."
|`sep_rom_entropy_lock_order_test` |`CHK-SRC-SEL-LOCK-AFTER-BRINGUP` |The first EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK AW follows the FIPS_LOCK AW, the SW_CMD_REQ AW, the CSRNG Instantiate, the seed ack and a SW_CMD_STS read; no SW_CMD_STS read follows it; it is the locking write and its B precedes the hand-off mret. Spec: "The ROM shall lock the TRNG source selection (`EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK`) once bring-up completes". Whether a lock blocks a later write is not graded; the order against the OTBN URND reseed is OBS only, because `rom.adoc` does not say whether that reseed must follow the lock.
|`sep_rom_no_fuse_program_test` |`CHK-NO-OTP-CHANGE` |The 256-word OTP bank at hand-off and at the end equals the bank at reset. Spec: "*the ROM burns no fuses*".
|`sep_rom_no_fuse_program_test` |`CHK-NO-PROGRAM-CMD` |No PROGRAM or PROGRAM_READ_BACK command on the eFuse shim tap from the monitor start to the end. Spec: "BL0 therefore has no fuse-programming path by design". The `bl0_state` commit directive is not graded: tbl:bl0_state has no field for it.
|`sep_rom_rsa_exponent_non_f4_refuse_test`, `sep_rom_rsa_exponent_non_f4_refuse_test_f4_signed` |`CHK-NON-F4-REFUSED` |The primary attempt ends with a MANIFEST_ERR= code, and the ROM then tries the backup; the spec names no code, so any code passes; this is the one exception to the credit rule that a refusal must carry the expected code (<<sep-rom-vplan-credit-rules>>). Spec: "a public key carrying any other exponent shall be rejected." The `_f4_signed` entry separates rejection from an ignored exponent (the key layout comes from the OCA implementation, because `rom.adoc` gives no exponent field).
|`sep_rom_rsa_key_direct_otbn_test` |`CHK-NO-KM-TO-OTBN` |Across the BL0 run every Key Manager key-path AW and AR valid and every engine sideload key valid stays a known 0, the Key Manager CPU fetch count stays constant, and the CPU makes no access to the mailbox aperture. Spec: "the Key Manager shall take no part in secure-boot signature verification."
|`sep_rom_rsa_key_direct_otbn_test` |`CHK-CPU-WRITES-MODULUS` |Before the EXECUTE command every word of the 384-byte modulus buffer takes a CPU write with OKAY, the DMEM write count grows only by the CPU writes, and the post-run read-back equals the modulus of the key that signed the accepted manifest. Spec: "The manifest's public key shall be delivered directly to OTBN". The exponent is not graded.
|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-ENCRYPTED-REFUSED` |The flash serves the primary then the backup manifest, and no MANIFEST_VALIDATED or PAYLOAD_VALIDATED word is reported. Spec: "a manifest marking its payload encrypted while the secure-boot decision is *off* shall be rejected".
|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-REFUSAL-CAUSE` |`bl0_state.error_code` at the verdict is `OCA_BOOT_ERR_BASE` combined with `OCA_FAIL_SECURE_BOOT_INVARIANT` or with `OCA_FAIL_ENCRYPTION_REQUIRES_SECURE_BOOT`. Spec: "(the `secure-boot invariant` check)"; PL-050 and the security inventory name `OCA_FAIL_SECURE_BOOT_INVARIANT`, while MAN-030, the pipeline figure and the code registry name `OCA_FAIL_ENCRYPTION_REQUIRES_SECURE_BOOT`, so the set both readings accept is graded.
|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-NO-DECRYPTION` |No DECRYPTION_START, DECRYPTION_END or DECRYPTION_FAILED word and no DECRYPT_OK line; in the manifest-attempt span no retirement at the decrypt, KDF or AES entry and no CPU access to the AES aperture. Spec: "Payload encryption shall require secure boot".
|`sep_rom_encryption_requires_secure_boot_test` |`CHK-TERMINAL` |ERROR MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED (0x0F010213) after the last SPI chip-select and before the last verdict, 0xDEADBEEF; no retirement leaves the ROM; 20,000 clk halt window. Spec (SEP-ROM-RETRY-030): "A failure anywhere in that chain fails the *slot*, not the boot, until no slot remains."
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_*` (4 entries) |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE` |When the decryption stage returns, no 16-byte window of the class key, the derived key or the KDF input, in byte order or word-reversed, is at any DCCM byte offset. Spec: "All key material (class key, derived key, KDF inputs) shall be zeroized (`explicit_memzero`) on every exit path from the decryption stage, success or failure." Graded as material not found, not as bytes written to zero.
|`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_*` (4 entries) |`CHK-NO-KEY-RESIDUE-AT-END` |The same search at the hand-off mret (success) or at the FAIL verdict (failures). Spec: as NO-KEY-RESIDUE.
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |`CHK-READ-RETRIED` |At least two read frames at the primary manifest address before the first backup frame, at least two at the backup address, and each slot's WARN SPI_OT_TRANSPORT_ERROR after its last frame. Spec: "A transport-level read fault shall be retried ... before being reported to the loader".
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |`CHK-ATTEMPTS-BOUNDED` |At most four read frames at each slot's manifest address. Spec: "a bounded number of times (OpenTitan driver: 3)".
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |`CHK-NO-PARTIAL-USE` |With the first 8 bytes of each faulted frame, the manifest magic included, in the buffer, the ROM does not act on them: no frame returns a byte outside the span of its slot's first frame, no WARN word other than SPI_OT_* from the first MANIFEST_LOAD_START to MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED, no MANIFEST_VALIDATED or PAYLOAD_VALIDATED word, and no OCA_BODY=, MANIFEST_OK or PAYLOAD_OK line. Spec: "never by accepting partial data."
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |`CHK-FALLBACK-OR-TERMINAL` |Every frame lies in the primary or backup slot span; a MANIFEST_LOAD_START word between the last primary and the first backup frame; after the last backup frame ERROR MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED (0x0F010213), then 0xDEADBEEF, no later frame, and a 20,000 clk halt. Spec: "a read that still fails is handled by slot fallback"; SEP-ROM-RETRY-010 "primary (`0x1000`) then backup (`0x41000`)"; SEP-ROM-RETRY-050 (b) to (d).
|`sep_rom_spi_retry_exhaust_test` |`CHK-LOAD-START-PER-ATTEMPT` |The number of MANIFEST_LOAD_START words equals the number of slots with read frames, and each such slot has exactly one after the previous slot's last frame and before its own first frame. Spec (tbl:stage_trace, [S23]): "`MANIFEST_LOAD_START` (per attempt)".
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-regression-groups]]
=== Regression Groups

[cols="1,2,3",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Tests |Purpose
|`rom_fw_smoke` |`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test`, `sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test`, `sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test` |Bring-up of the SMC-SRAM, OpenTitan SPI DMA and PIO, and signed boot paths, and of the hand-off magic. Run it first after a ROM or toolchain change
|`rom_fw` |All 237 entries of `testlists/rom_fw.toml` |The full Boot ROM firmware regression and the credit set of this plan
|===

[source,bash]
----
python3 tools/dv/run_dv.py --dut sep --items rom_fw_smoke
python3 tools/dv/run_dv.py --dut sep --items rom_fw --regress \
  --sim-jobs 8 --build-jobs 24
----

The `c_compile` stage builds both firmware profiles. It needs a
picolibc-enabled RISC-V GCC or `scripts/docker-run.sh`, and the
`tt-oca-manifest` submodule:

[source,bash]
----
git submodule update --init hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/tools/tt-oca-manifest
----

Keep `--sim-jobs` near 8, because the threaded VeeR EL2 model can stall near
reset. Test timeouts are 1 to 6 hours of wall time.

[[sep-rom-vplan-known-limitations]]
=== Known Limitations

[cols="2,3",options="header"]
|===
|Limitation |Impact
|No CI tier runs `rom_fw` |Boot ROM evidence comes from a local run on a host with the toolchain
|Memory-repair gate |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030` names the `mem_repair_bypass` strap (`STRAPS_LO[13]`) as the only escape. `vector.S` also gates on the MBIST result, which `SEP-ROM-SMC-030` does not name; `sep_firmware_mbist_only_fail_test` follows `vector.S` for this behaviour
|PL-080 AES idle-timeout exits |`sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_init_fail` and `sep_rom_decrypt_exit_zeroize_test_aes_dec_fail` reach the AES failure exits through tb hooks at the first driver check. The ROM's bounded AES idle waits, which return through the same exits, are not executed in simulation
|`SEP-ROM-HOFF-050` has no test |`rom.adoc` says the gate ledger is not implemented and defines no observable output, so no test maps to the requirement
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-formal]]
=== Formal Verification Plan

The Boot ROM has no formal flow.

[[sep-rom-vplan-checklist]]
=== Plan Checklist

[cols="4,1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Item |State |Note
|Every test in the summary has a procedure group |Yes |237 tests in 35 groups
|Every documented test exists in `rom_fw.toml` and in the `rom_fw` group |Yes |Checked against both
|Every test has at least one checker with a PASS record |Yes |Static check of the test source: 0 tests without one
|`rom_fw` has an `expected_count` |No |`rom_fw_smoke` has one. `rom_fw` does not, so a dropped entry does not fail the testlist load
|The ROM owner reviewed the requirement mappings |No |Pending owner review
|===
